        paddingVertical: Spacing.sm, paddingHorizontal: Spacing.md,
        borderBottomWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(83,96,132,0.08)',
        flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: Spacing.sm,
    },
    checkRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 },
    checkbox: {
        width: 22, height: 22, borderRadius: 7, borderWidth: 2,
        borderColor: 'rgba(83,96,132,0.25)', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
    },
    checkboxOn: { backgroundColor: '#ff6600', borderColor: '#ff6600' },
    extraLabel: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', color: '#8B7CC8' },
    extraInputs: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 },
    pctToggle: {
        flexDirection: 'row', backgroundColor: 'rgba(83,96,132,0.08)',
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.sm, overflow: 'hidden',
    },
    pctBtn: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 6 },
    pctBtnOn: { backgroundColor: '#ff6600' },
    pctTxt: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold', color: '#8B7CC8' },
    pctTxtOn: { color: '#fff' },
    extraInput: {
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(83,96,132,0.06)', borderRadius: BorderRadius.sm,
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.sm, paddingVertical: 6, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium',
        color: '#1A1040', fontSize: FontSize.sm, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.1)',
        width: 70, textAlign: 'center',
    },
    breakdown: {
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.88)', borderRadius: BorderRadius.md,
        padding: Spacing.md, marginTop: Spacing.md,
        borderWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.08)',
    },
    brow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 4 },
    blbl: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', color: '#4B4570' },
    bval: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold', color: '#1A1040' },
    btotalRow: {
        borderTopWidth: 1, borderColor: 'rgba(83,96,132,0.1)',
        paddingTop: Spacing.sm, marginTop: Spacing.xs,
    },
    btotalLbl: { fontSize: FontSize.md, fontFamily: 'Poppins_800ExtraBold', color: '#1A1040' },
    btotalVal: { fontSize: FontSize.md, fontFamily: 'Poppins_800ExtraBold', color: '#ff6600' },

    scanBtn: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
        gap: Spacing.sm, marginTop: Spacing.lg,
        padding: Spacing.md, borderRadius: BorderRadius.full,
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.08)',
    },
    scanTxt: { fontSize: FontSize.md, fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold', color: '#ff6600' },
    saveBtn: {
        backgroundColor: '#ff6600', paddingVertical: Spacing.lg,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.full, alignItems: 'center', marginTop: Spacing.xl,
        shadowColor: '#ff6600', shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 6 },
        shadowOpacity: 0.35, shadowRadius: 12, elevation: Platform.OS === 'android' ? 0 : 6,
    },
    saveTxt: { color: '#fff', fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold', fontSize: FontSize.lg },

    // Recurring styles
    recWrapper: { marginTop: Spacing.md },
    repeatToggleRow: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', padding: Spacing.md,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.md, borderWidth: 1,
    },
    repeatLabel: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold' },
    repeatSublabel: { fontSize: FontSize.xs, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', marginTop: 2 },
    recurringOptionsCard: {
        marginTop: Spacing.sm, padding: Spacing.md, borderRadius: BorderRadius.md,
        borderWidth: 1,
    },
    recSectionTitle: { fontSize: FontSize.xs, fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold', marginBottom: Spacing.xs, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: 0.5 },
    recFreqRow: { gap: Spacing.xs, paddingVertical: 2 },
    recChip: {
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.md, paddingVertical: Spacing.xs,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.full, borderWidth: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
    },
    recChipText: { fontSize: FontSize.xs },
    recModeRow: {
        flexDirection: 'row', borderRadius: BorderRadius.md, padding: 3, gap: 4, marginTop: 2,
    },
    recModeBtn: {
        flex: 1, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
        gap: 5, paddingVertical: Spacing.sm, borderRadius: BorderRadius.sm,
    },
    recModeBtnActive: { shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 }, shadowOpacity: 0.2, shadowRadius: 4, elevation: 2 },
    recModeText: { fontSize: FontSize.xs, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium' },
    recModeTextActive: { fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold' },
    recHintText: { fontSize: FontSize.xs, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', marginTop: Spacing.sm, lineHeight: 16 },




});



================ FILE: app/assets.js ================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
    Platform, View, Text, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity, TextInput, Modal, Alert, StyleSheet, KeyboardAvoidingView
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import { formatCurrency, currencies, getTopCurrencies } from '../utils/currencies';
import { generateId } from '../utils/helpers';
import { accountTypes, getAccountTypeById } from '../utils/categories';
import { Colors, Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import DonutChart from '../components/DonutChart';


import FastInput from '../components/FastInput';
// --------------------------------------------


// ── Color palette users can pick for their cards ──────────────
const CARD_COLORS = [
    '#ff6600', '#3B82F6', '#1E293B', '#0F4C75',
    '#059669', '#D97706', '#DC2626', '#DB2777',
    '#7C3AED', '#0891B2', '#9D174D', '#1E3A5F',
];

const DEFAULT_COLORS = ['#ff6600', '#1E3A8A', '#1E293B', '#0F4C75', '#7C3AED', '#9D174D'];

function getCardColor(asset, index) {
    return asset.color || DEFAULT_COLORS[index % DEFAULT_COLORS.length];
}

export default function Assets() {
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const router = useRouter();
    const { assets, currency, addAsset, updateAsset, deleteAsset, addTransaction, exchangeRates, convertCurrency } = useApp();

    const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
    const [showTransfer, setShowTransfer] = useState(false);
    const [editAsset, setEditAsset] = useState(null);
    const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(null);
    const [showBreakdownModal, setShowBreakdownModal] = useState(false);
    const [showAllCurrencies, setShowAllCurrencies] = useState(false);

    // Form state
    const [name, setName] = useState('');
    const [type, setType] = useState('bank');
    const [balance, setBalance] = useState('');
    const [color, setColor] = useState('#ff6600');
    const [assetCurrency, setAssetCurrency] = useState(currency);

    // Transfer state
    const [fromId, setFromId] = useState('');
    const [toId, setToId] = useState('');
    const [transferAmount, setTransferAmount] = useState('');

    const totalBalance = assets.reduce((s, a) => s + convertCurrency(a.balance || 0, a.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates), 0);

    // Per-currency subtotals for the breakdown chips
    const currencyBreakdown = useMemo(() => {
        const groups = {};
        assets.forEach(a => {
            const cur = a.currency || currency;
            if (!groups[cur]) groups[cur] = 0;
            groups[cur] += a.balance || 0;
        });
        return Object.entries(groups)
            .map(([cur, total]) => ({
                cur,
                originalTotal: total,
                convertedTotal: convertCurrency(total, cur, currency, exchangeRates),
            }))
            .sort((a, b) => b.convertedTotal - a.convertedTotal);
    }, [assets, currency, exchangeRates]);
    const hasMixedCurrencies = currencyBreakdown.length > 1;

    const [showAllocation, setShowAllocation] = useState(false);

    const assetPieData = useMemo(() => {
        return [...assets].sort((a, b) => b.balance - a.balance).map((a, i) => ({
            label: a.name,
            value: Math.max(convertCurrency(a.balance || 0, a.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates), 0),
            color: getCardColor(a, i),
            id: a.id
        })).filter(x => x.value > 0);
    }, [assets, currency, exchangeRates]);

    const openAdd = () => {
        setEditAsset(null);
        setName('');
        setType('bank');
        setBalance('');
        setColor('#ff6600');
        setAssetCurrency(currency);
        setShowAllCurrencies(false);
        setShowModal(true);
    };

    const openEdit = (a) => {
        setEditAsset(a);
        setName(a.name);
        setType(a.type);
        setBalance(String(a.balance || 0));
        setColor(a.color || '#ff6600');
        setAssetCurrency(a.currency || currency);
        setShowAllCurrencies(false);
        setShowModal(true);
    };

    const handleSave = async () => {
        if (!name.trim()) {
            Alert.alert('Error', 'Please enter an account name');
            return;
        }
        const bal = parseFloat(balance) || 0;
        const cur = (assetCurrency || currency).trim().toUpperCase();
        if (editAsset) {
            await updateAsset({ ...editAsset, name, type, balance: bal, color, currency: cur });
        } else {
            await addAsset({ id: generateId(), name, type, balance: bal, color, currency: cur });
        }
        setShowModal(false);
    };

    const handleTransfer = async () => {
        const from = assets.find(a => a.id === fromId);
        const to = assets.find(a => a.id === toId);
        if (!from || !to) { Alert.alert('Error', 'Select two different accounts'); return; }
        const fromCur = from.currency || currency;
        const toCur = to.currency || currency;
        const amt = parseFloat(transferAmount);
        if (!amt || amt <= 0) { Alert.alert('Error', 'Enter a valid amount'); return; }
        if (fromId === toId) { Alert.alert('Error', 'Select two different accounts'); return; }

        const destAmt = fromCur !== toCur
            ? convertCurrency(amt, fromCur, toCur, exchangeRates)
            : amt;

        await updateAsset({ ...from, balance: (from.balance || 0) - amt });
        await updateAsset({ ...to, balance: (to.balance || 0) + destAmt });
        await addTransaction({
            id: generateId(),
            type: 'move',
            amount: amt,
            category: 'transfer',
            description: `${from.name} → ${to.name}`,
            date: new Date().toISOString(),
            items: [],
            photos: [],
            paymentMethod: 'transfer',
            assetId: null,
            fromAssetId: from.id,
            toAssetId: to.id,
            currency: fromCur,
        });
        setShowTransfer(false);
        setTransferAmount('');
        setFromId('');
        setToId('');
    };

    const handleDelete = (id) => {
        Alert.alert('Delete Account', 'Are you sure you want to delete this account?', [
            { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
            { text: 'Delete', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => { deleteAsset(id); setSelectedId(null); } },
        ]);
    };

    return (
        <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>

            {/* Header */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
                    <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: 'transparent' }]} onPress={() => router.back()}>
                        <Ionicons name="chevron-down" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Assets</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.headerBtns}>
                    {assets.length >= 2 && (
                        <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.transferBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]} onPress={() => setShowTransfer(true)}>
                            <Ionicons name="swap-horizontal" size={16} color={colors.primary} />
                            <Text style={[styles.transferBtnText, { color: colors.primary }]}>Transfer</Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                    <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.addBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]} onPress={openAdd}>
                        <Ionicons name="add" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
            </View>

            {/* Total card */}
            <TouchableOpacity activeOpacity={0.8} onPress={() => setShowAllocation(!showAllocation)} style={[styles.totalCard, { backgroundColor: 'transparent', borderColor: 'transparent' }]}>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                    <Text style={[styles.totalLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Total Balance</Text>
                    {hasMixedCurrencies && (
                        <TouchableOpacity
                            onPress={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setShowBreakdownModal(true); }}
                            hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
                        >
                            <Ionicons name="information-circle-outline" size={16} color={colors.textMuted} />
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                </View>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                    <Text style={[styles.totalAmount, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{formatCurrency(totalBalance, currency)}</Text>
                    <Ionicons name={showAllocation ? "chevron-up" : "chevron-down"} size={20} color={colors.textSecondary} style={{ marginTop: 4 }} />
                </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {showAllocation && assetPieData.length > 0 && (
                <View style={styles.allocationContainer}>
                    <DonutChart
                        data={assetPieData}
                        size={180}
                        strokeWidth={28}
                        centerLabel="Total Assets"
                        centerValue={formatCurrency(totalBalance, currency)}
                        colors={colors}
                    />
                    <View style={styles.allocationLegend}>
                        {assetPieData.map((item) => {
                            const pct = ((item.value / totalBalance) * 100).toFixed(1);
                            return (
                                <View key={item.id} style={[styles.allocationLegendRow, { borderBottomColor: colors.border }]}>
                                    <View style={styles.allocationLegendLeft}>
                                        <View style={[styles.allocationDot, { backgroundColor: item.color }]} />
                                        <Text style={[styles.allocationLegendName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{item.label}</Text>
                                    </View>
                                    <View style={styles.allocationLegendRight}>
                                        <Text style={[styles.allocationLegendVal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{formatCurrency(item.value, currency)}</Text>
                                        <Text style={[styles.allocationLegendPct, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{pct}%</Text>
                                    </View>
                                </View>
                            );
                        })}
                    </View>
                </View>
            )}

            {/* Asset cards list */}
            <ScrollView
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
                contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
            >
                {assets.length === 0 ? (
                    <View style={styles.emptyState}>
                        <Ionicons name="card-outline" size={52} color={colors.textMuted} />
                        <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>No accounts yet</Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.emptyDesc, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Add your bank accounts, wallets, and more</Text>
                    </View>
                ) : (
                    assets.map((a, index) => {
                        const at = getAccountTypeById(a.type);
                        const cardColor = getCardColor(a, index);
                        const isSelected = selectedId === a.id;

                        return (
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                key={a.id}
                                activeOpacity={0.88}
                                onPress={() => setSelectedId(isSelected ? null : a.id)}
                                style={[styles.atmCard, { backgroundColor: cardColor, shadowColor: isDark ? '#000' : '#1A1040' }]}
                            >
                                {/* Chip + type icon row */}
                                <View style={styles.atmCardTop}>
                                    <View style={styles.chip}>
                                        <View style={styles.chipLine} />
                                        <View style={styles.chipLine} />
                                    </View>
                                    <Ionicons name={at.icon} size={22} color="rgba(255,255,255,0.55)" />
                                </View>

                                {/* Name */}
                                <Text style={styles.atmCardName} numberOfLines={1}>{a.name}</Text>

                                {/* Balance */}
                                <Text style={[styles.atmCardBalance, { color: a.balance >= 0 ? '#fff' : '#FFB3BA' }]}>
                                    {formatCurrency(a.balance, a.currency || currency)}
                                </Text>

                                {/* Footer: type + actions (visible when selected) */}
                                <View style={styles.atmCardFooter}>
                                    <Text style={styles.atmCardType}>{at.name}</Text>
                                    {isSelected && (
                                        <View style={styles.atmActions}>
                                            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.atmActionBtn} onPress={() => openEdit(a)}>
                                                <Ionicons name="create-outline" size={13} color="#fff" />
                                                <Text style={styles.atmActionTxt}>Edit</Text>
                                            </TouchableOpacity>
                                            <TouchableOpacity
                                                style={[styles.atmActionBtn, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(220,38,38,0.75)' }]}
                                                onPress={() => handleDelete(a.id)}
                                            >
                                                <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={13} color="#fff" />
                                                <Text style={styles.atmActionTxt}>Delete</Text>
                                            </TouchableOpacity>
                                        </View>
                                    )}
                                </View>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        );
                    })
                )}
            </ScrollView>

            {/* ── Add / Edit Modal ──────────────────────────────────── */}
            <Modal visible={showModal} animationType="slide" transparent onRequestClose={() => setShowModal(false)}>
                <KeyboardAvoidingView
                    style={{ flex: 1 }}
                    behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
                >
                    <View style={[styles.modalOverlay, { backgroundColor: colors.overlay }]}>
                        <View style={[styles.modalBox, { backgroundColor: colors.modalBg }]}>
                            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} bounces={false}>
                                {/* Modal header */}
                                <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
                                    <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{editAsset ? 'Edit Account' : 'New Account'}</Text>
                                    <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => requestAnimationFrame(() => setShowModal(false))}>
                                        <Ionicons name="close" size={24} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                                    </TouchableOpacity>
                                </View>

                                {/* Account name */}
                                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Account Name</Text>
                                <FastInput
                                    style={[styles.input, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder, color: colors.textPrimary }]}
                                    placeholder="e.g. BCA Savings"
                                    placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                                    value={name}
                                    onChangeText={setName}
                                />

                                {/* Account type */}
                                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Account Type</Text>
                                <View style={styles.typeGrid}>
                                    {accountTypes.map(t => (
                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                            key={t.id}
                                            style={[styles.typeOption, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder }, type === t.id && [styles.typeOptionActive, { borderColor: colors.primary, backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]]}
                                            onPress={() => setType(t.id)}
                                        >
                                            <Ionicons
                                                name={t.icon}
                                                size={22}
                                                color={type === t.id ? colors.primary : colors.textMuted}
                                            />
                                            <Text style={[styles.typeText, { color: colors.textMuted }, type === t.id && [styles.typeTextActive, { color: colors.primary }]]}>
                                                {t.name}
                                            </Text>
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    ))}
                                </View>

                                {/* Account Currency Picker */}
                                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Account Currency</Text>
                                <View style={styles.currencyGrid}>
                                    {(showAllCurrencies ? currencies : getTopCurrencies(currency, assetCurrency)).map(c => (
                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                            key={c.code}
                                            style={[
                                                styles.currencyOption,
                                                { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder },
                                                assetCurrency === c.code && [styles.currencyActive, { borderColor: colors.primary, backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }],
                                            ]}
                                            onPress={() => setAssetCurrency(c.code)}
                                            activeOpacity={0.8}
                                        >
                                            <Text style={[styles.currencyCode, { color: colors.textSecondary }, assetCurrency === c.code && [styles.currencyCodeActive, { color: colors.primary }]]}>{c.code}</Text>
                                            <Text style={[styles.currencySymbol, { color: colors.textMuted }, assetCurrency === c.code && [styles.currencySymbolActive, { color: colors.primary }]]}>{c.symbol}</Text>
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    ))}
                                    {!showAllCurrencies && (
                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                            style={[styles.currencyOption, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder }]}
                                            onPress={() => setShowAllCurrencies(true)}>
                                            <Ionicons name="ellipsis-horizontal" size={20} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                                            <Text style={[styles.currencySymbol, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>Other</Text>
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    )}
                                </View>

                                {/* Balance */}
                                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Balance ({assetCurrency})</Text>
                                <FastInput
                                    style={[styles.input, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder, color: colors.textPrimary }]}
                                    placeholder="0"
                                    placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                                    value={balance}
                                    onChangeText={setBalance}
                                    keyboardType="numeric"
                                />

                                {/* Card color */}
                                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Card Color</Text>
                                <View style={styles.colorPicker}>
                                    {CARD_COLORS.map(c => (
                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                            key={c}
                                            style={[
                                                styles.colorSwatch,
                                                { backgroundColor: c },
                                                color === c && [styles.colorSwatchSelected, { borderColor: isDark ? '#fff' : '#1A1040' }],
                                            ]}
                                            onPress={() => setColor(c)}
                                        >
                                            {color === c && (
                                                <Ionicons name="checkmark" size={16} color="#fff" />
                                            )}
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    ))}
                                </View>

                                {/* Preview */}
                                <View style={[styles.cardPreview, { backgroundColor: color }]}>
                                    <View style={styles.previewTop}>
                                        <View style={styles.chip}>
                                            <View style={styles.chipLine} />
                                            <View style={styles.chipLine} />
                                        </View>
                                        <Ionicons name={getAccountTypeById(type).icon} size={20} color="rgba(255,255,255,0.55)" />
                                    </View>
                                    <Text style={styles.previewName} numberOfLines={1}>
                                        {name || 'Account Name'}
                                    </Text>
                                    <Text style={styles.previewBalance}>
                                        {formatCurrency(parseFloat(balance) || 0, assetCurrency || currency)}
                                    </Text>
                                    <Text style={styles.previewType}>{getAccountTypeById(type).name}</Text>
                                </View>

                                <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.saveBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]} onPress={handleSave}>
                                    <Text style={styles.saveBtnText}>{editAsset ? 'Update Account' : 'Add Account'}</Text>
                                </TouchableOpacity>

                                <View style={{ height: 24 }} />
                            </ScrollView>
                        </View>
                    </View>
                </KeyboardAvoidingView>
            </Modal>

            {/* ── Transfer Modal ────────────────────────────────────── */}
            <Modal visible={showTransfer} animationType="slide" transparent onRequestClose={() => setShowTransfer(false)}>
                <KeyboardAvoidingView style={{ flex: 1 }} behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}>
                    <View style={[styles.modalOverlay, { backgroundColor: colors.overlay }]}>
                        <View style={[styles.modalBox, { backgroundColor: colors.modalBg, maxHeight: '90%' }]}>
                            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
                                <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Transfer Funds</Text>
                                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => requestAnimationFrame(() => setShowTransfer(false))}>
                                    <Ionicons name="close" size={24} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                                </TouchableOpacity>
                            </View>

                            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 24 }}>

                                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>From</Text>
                                <View style={styles.accountPicker}>
                                    {assets.map(a => (
                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                            key={a.id}
                                            style={[styles.accountOption, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder }, fromId === a.id && [styles.accountOptionActive, { borderColor: colors.primary, backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]]}
                                            onPress={() => setFromId(a.id)}
                                        >
                                            <Text style={[styles.accountOptionText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{a.name}</Text>
                                            <Text style={[styles.accountOptionBal, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{formatCurrency(a.balance, a.currency || currency)}</Text>
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    ))}
                                </View>

                                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>To</Text>
                                <View style={styles.accountPicker}>
                                    {assets.filter(a => a.id !== fromId).map(a => (
                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                            key={a.id}
                                            style={[styles.accountOption, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder }, toId === a.id && [styles.accountOptionActive, { borderColor: colors.primary, backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]]}
                                            onPress={() => setToId(a.id)}
                                        >
                                            <Text style={[styles.accountOptionText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{a.name}</Text>
                                            <Text style={[styles.accountOptionBal, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{formatCurrency(a.balance, a.currency || currency)}</Text>
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    ))}
                                </View>

                                <Text style={[styles.inputLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Amount ({currency})</Text>
                                <FastInput
                                    style={[styles.input, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder, color: colors.textPrimary }]}
                                    placeholder="0"
                                    placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                                    value={transferAmount}
                                    onChangeText={setTransferAmount}
                                    keyboardType="numeric"
                                />

                                <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.saveBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]} onPress={handleTransfer}>
                                    <Text style={styles.saveBtnText}>Transfer</Text>
                                </TouchableOpacity>
                            </ScrollView>
                        </View>
                    </View>
                </KeyboardAvoidingView>
            </Modal>

            {/* Currency Breakdown Modal */}
            <Modal visible={showBreakdownModal} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={() => setShowBreakdownModal(false)}>
                <View style={styles.smOverlay}>
                    <View style={[styles.smBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>

                        {/* Header */}
                        <View style={styles.smHeader}>
                            <View style={[styles.smIconWrap, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.12)' }]}>
                                <Ionicons name="wallet" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                            </View>
                            <Text style={[styles.smTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Balance Breakdown</Text>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                onPress={() => setShowBreakdownModal(false)}
                                style={[styles.smClose, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}
                            >
                                <Ionicons name="close" size={18} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>

                        {/* Per-currency rows */}
                        {currencyBreakdown.map((item) => (
                            <View key={item.cur} style={[styles.smRow, { borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                                <View style={[styles.smCurBadge, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}>
                                    <Text style={[styles.smCurCode, { color: colors.primary }]}>{item.cur}</Text>
                                </View>
                                <View style={styles.smAmounts}>
                                    <Text style={[styles.smOriginal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                                        {formatCurrency(item.originalTotal, item.cur)}
                                    </Text>
                                    {item.cur !== currency && (
                                        <View style={styles.smConvRow}>
                                            <Ionicons name="return-down-forward" size={13} color={colors.textMuted} />
                                            <Text style={[styles.smConverted, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                                                {formatCurrency(item.convertedTotal, currency)}
                                            </Text>
                                        </View>
                                    )}
                                </View>
                            </View>
                        ))}

                        {/* Total */}
                        <View style={[styles.smTotalRow, { borderTopColor: colors.border }]}>
                            <Text style={[styles.smTotalLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Total in {currency}</Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.smTotalVal, { color: colors.primary }]}>
                                {formatCurrency(totalBalance, currency)}
                            </Text>
                        </View>

                        {/* Done button */}
                        <View style={styles.smActions}>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                style={[styles.smDoneBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}
                                onPress={() => setShowBreakdownModal(false)}
                            >
                                <Text style={styles.smDoneTxt}>Done</Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>

                    </View>
                </View>
            </Modal>
        </View>
    );
}

================ FILE: app/calendar-view.js ================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { View, Text, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity, StyleSheet, Modal, useWindowDimensions } from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { format, startOfMonth, endOfMonth, startOfWeek, endOfWeek, addDays, addMonths, subMonths, isSameMonth, isSameDay } from 'date-fns';
import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import { formatCurrency } from '../utils/currencies';
import { formatTime } from '../utils/helpers';
import { getCategoryById } from '../utils/categories';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';

const WEEKDAYS = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];

function getHeatColor(amount, maxAmount, colors, isDark) {
    if (amount === 0) return 'transparent';
    const intensity = Math.min(amount / (maxAmount || 1), 1);
    if (intensity < 0.25) return isDark ? 'rgba(35,212,153,0.25)' : 'rgba(16,185,129,0.18)';
    if (intensity < 0.5) return isDark ? 'rgba(240,224,96,0.3)' : 'rgba(245,158,11,0.2)';
    if (intensity < 0.75) return isDark ? 'rgba(255,133,148,0.35)' : 'rgba(249,115,22,0.25)';
    return isDark ? 'rgba(255,102,0,0.45)' : 'rgba(239,68,68,0.3)';
}

export default function CalendarView() {
    const router = useRouter();
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const { transactions, currency, exchangeRates, convertCurrency } = useApp();
    const [currentMonth, setCurrentMonth] = useState(new Date());
    const [selectedDay, setSelectedDay] = useState(null);
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();

    const cellSize = useMemo(() => Math.floor((screenWidth - Spacing.lg * 2 - 12) / 7), [screenWidth]);

    // Build calendar grid
    const calendarData = useMemo(() => {
        const monthStart = startOfMonth(currentMonth);
        const monthEnd = endOfMonth(currentMonth);
        const calStart = startOfWeek(monthStart, { weekStartsOn: 1 });
        const calEnd = endOfWeek(monthEnd, { weekStartsOn: 1 });

        // Index transactions by day
        const dailyTotals = {};
        transactions.forEach(t => {
            if (t.type !== 'spending') return;
            const dayKey = format(new Date(t.date), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
            if (!dailyTotals[dayKey]) dailyTotals[dayKey] = { spending: 0, income: 0, count: 0, transactions: [] };
            dailyTotals[dayKey].spending += Math.abs(convertCurrency(t.amount, t.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates));
            dailyTotals[dayKey].count += 1;
            dailyTotals[dayKey].transactions.push(t);
        });
        transactions.forEach(t => {
            if (t.type !== 'income') return;
            const dayKey = format(new Date(t.date), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
            if (!dailyTotals[dayKey]) dailyTotals[dayKey] = { spending: 0, income: 0, count: 0, transactions: [] };
            dailyTotals[dayKey].income += Math.abs(convertCurrency(t.amount, t.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates));
            dailyTotals[dayKey].count += 1;
            dailyTotals[dayKey].transactions.push(t);
        });

        const weeks = [];
        let day = calStart;
        let maxSpend = 0;

        while (day <= calEnd) {
            const week = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
                const dayKey = format(day, 'yyyy-MM-dd');
                const data = dailyTotals[dayKey] || { spending: 0, income: 0, count: 0, transactions: [] };
                if (data.spending > maxSpend) maxSpend = data.spending;
                week.push({
                    date: new Date(day),
                    dayKey,
                    dayNum: day.getDate(),
                    isCurrentMonth: isSameMonth(day, currentMonth),
                    isToday: isSameDay(day, new Date()),
                    ...data,
                });
                day = addDays(day, 1);
            }
            weeks.push(week);
        }

        return { weeks, maxSpend, dailyTotals };
    }, [currentMonth, transactions, currency, exchangeRates]);

    // Monthly summary
    const monthSummary = useMemo(() => {
        const monthStart = startOfMonth(currentMonth);
        const monthEnd = endOfMonth(currentMonth);
        let income = 0, spending = 0;
        transactions.forEach(t => {
            const d = new Date(t.date);
            if (d >= monthStart && d <= monthEnd) {
                const amt = Math.abs(convertCurrency(t.amount, t.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates));
                if (t.type === 'income') income += amt;
                else if (t.type === 'spending') spending += amt;
            }
        });
        return { income, spending, net: income - spending };
    }, [currentMonth, transactions, currency, exchangeRates]);

    const selectedDayData = selectedDay
        ? calendarData.weeks.flat().find(d => d.dayKey === selectedDay)
        : null;

    return (
        <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
            <Svg key={colors.background} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject} width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} pointerEvents="none">
                <Defs>
                    <SvgGrad id="bgGrad" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                        <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                    </SvgGrad>
                </Defs>
                <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} fill="url(#bgGrad)" />
            </Svg>

            {/* Header */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => router.back()} style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]}>
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Spending Calendar</Text>
                <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
            </View>

            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 40 }}>
                {/* Month Summary */}
                <View style={[styles.summaryCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                    <View style={styles.summaryItem}>
                        <Text style={[styles.summaryAmount, { color: colors.emerald }]}>+{formatCurrency(monthSummary.income, currency)}</Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.summaryLabel, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>Income</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.summaryDivider, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />
                    <View style={styles.summaryItem}>
                        <Text style={[styles.summaryAmount, { color: colors.rose }]}>-{formatCurrency(monthSummary.spending, currency)}</Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.summaryLabel, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>Spending</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.summaryDivider, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />
                    <View style={styles.summaryItem}>
                        <Text style={[styles.summaryAmount, { color: monthSummary.net >= 0 ? colors.emerald : colors.rose }]}>
                            {monthSummary.net >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{formatCurrency(monthSummary.net, currency)}
                        </Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.summaryLabel, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>Net</Text>
                    </View>
                </View>

                {/* Month Navigator */}
                <View style={styles.monthNav}>
                    <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.navArrow, { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]} onPress={() => setCurrentMonth(subMonths(currentMonth, 1))}>
                        <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={18} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <Text style={[styles.monthLabel, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                        {format(currentMonth, 'MMMM yyyy')}
                    </Text>
                    <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.navArrow, { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]} onPress={() => setCurrentMonth(addMonths(currentMonth, 1))}>
                        <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>

                {/* Calendar Grid */}
                <View style={[styles.calendarCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                    {/* Weekday Headers */}
                    <View style={styles.weekdayRow}>
                        {WEEKDAYS.map(day => (
                            <View key={day} style={[styles.weekdayCell, { width: cellSize }]}>
                                <Text style={[styles.weekdayText, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>{day}</Text>
                            </View>
                        ))}
                    </View>

                    {/* Day Cells */}
                    {calendarData.weeks.map((week, wi) => (
                        <View key={wi} style={styles.weekRow}>
                            {week.map(day => {
                                const isSelected = selectedDay === day.dayKey;
                                const heatColor = getHeatColor(day.spending, calendarData.maxSpend, colors, isDark);
                                return (
                                    <TouchableOpacity
                                        key={day.dayKey}
                                        style={[
                                            styles.dayCell,
                                            { width: cellSize, height: cellSize },
                                            day.isCurrentMonth && heatColor !== 'transparent' && { backgroundColor: heatColor },
                                            isSelected && { borderColor: colors.primary, borderWidth: 2 },
                                            day.isToday && !isSelected && { borderColor: colors.primary, borderWidth: 1 },
                                        ]}
                                        onPress={() => setSelectedDay(isSelected ? null : day.dayKey)}
                                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                                    >
                                        <Text style={[
                                            styles.dayNum,
                                            { color: day.isCurrentMonth ? colors.textPrimary : colors.textMuted },
                                            day.isToday && { color: colors.primary, fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold' },
                                        ]}>
                                            {day.dayNum}
                                        </Text>
                                        {day.spending > 0 && day.isCurrentMonth && (
                                            <Text style={[styles.dayAmount, { color: colors.rose }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                                {Math.abs(day.spending) >= 1000 ? `${(day.spending / 1000).toFixed(0)}k` : Math.round(day.spending)}
                                            </Text>
                                        )}
                                    </TouchableOpacity>
                                );
                            })}
                        </View>
                    ))}
                </View>

                {/* Legend */}
                <View style={styles.legendRow}>
                    <Text style={[styles.legendLabel, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>Low</Text>
                    <View style={[styles.legendBox, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(35,212,153,0.25)' : 'rgba(16,185,129,0.18)' }]} />
                    <View style={[styles.legendBox, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(240,224,96,0.3)' : 'rgba(245,158,11,0.2)' }]} />
                    <View style={[styles.legendBox, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,133,148,0.35)' : 'rgba(249,115,22,0.25)' }]} />
                    <View style={[styles.legendBox, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,102,0,0.45)' : 'rgba(239,68,68,0.3)' }]} />
                    <Text style={[styles.legendLabel, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>High</Text>
                </View>

                {/* Selected Day Detail */}
                {selectedDayData && selectedDayData.transactions.length > 0 && (
                    <View style={[styles.dayDetailCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                        <View style={styles.dayDetailHeader}>
                            <Text style={[styles.dayDetailTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                                {format(selectedDayData.date, 'EEEE, MMM dd')}
                            </Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.dayDetailCount, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>
                                {selectedDayData.transactions.length} transaction{selectedDayData.transactions.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
                            </Text>
                        </View>

                        <View style={[styles.dayDetailSummary, { borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                            {selectedDayData.income > 0 && (
                                <Text style={[styles.dayDetailStat, { color: colors.emerald }]}>+{formatCurrency(selectedDayData.income, currency)}</Text>
                            )}
                            {selectedDayData.spending > 0 && (
                                <Text style={[styles.dayDetailStat, { color: colors.rose }]}>-{formatCurrency(selectedDayData.spending, currency)}</Text>
                            )}
                        </View>

                        {selectedDayData.transactions
                            .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date))
                            .map(tx => {
                                const cat = tx.type === 'move'
                                    ? { name: 'Transfer', icon: 'swap-horizontal', color: '#6A6FF8' }
                                    : getCategoryById(tx.category);
                                const isIncome = tx.type === 'income';
                                return (
                                    <View key={tx.id} style={[styles.dayTxRow, { borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                                        <View style={[styles.dayTxIcon, { backgroundColor: isDark ? `${cat.color}33` : `${cat.color}18` }]}>
                                            <Ionicons name={cat.icon} size={16} color={cat.color} />
                                        </View>
                                        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                                            <Text style={[styles.dayTxName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                                {tx.description || cat.name}
                                            </Text>
                                            <Text style={[styles.dayTxTime, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>{formatTime(tx.date)}</Text>
                                        </View>
                                        <Text style={[styles.dayTxAmount, { color: isIncome ? colors.emerald : colors.rose }]}>
                                            {isIncome ? '+' : '-'}{formatCurrency(Math.abs(tx.amount), tx.currency || currency)}
                                        </Text>
                                    </View>
                                );
                            })}
                    </View>
                )}
            </ScrollView>
        </View>
    );
}
