================ FILE: app/category-transactions.js ================

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Platform, View, Text, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity,
    StyleSheet, Modal, Alert, useWindowDimensions
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { format, addDays, subDays, addWeeks, subWeeks, addMonths, subMonths } from 'date-fns';
import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import { formatCurrency } from '../utils/currencies';
import { filterTransactionsByPeriod, getDateRange, formatTime } from '../utils/helpers';
import { getCategoryById, normalizeCategoryId } from '../utils/categories';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';

/* ─── Date Picker Modal ─── */
function DatePickerModal({ visible, value, onConfirm, onClose, colors }) {
    const months = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
    const [month, setMonth] = useState(0);
    const [day, setDay] = useState(1);
    const [year, setYear] = useState(new Date().getFullYear());

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!visible) return;
        const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
        setMonth(date.getMonth());
        setDay(date.getDate());
        setYear(date.getFullYear());
    }, [visible, value]);

    const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();

    const handleConfirm = () => {
        const safeDay = Math.min(day, daysInMonth);
        onConfirm(new Date(year, month, safeDay));
        onClose();
    };

    return (
        <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={onClose}>
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.modalOverlay} activeOpacity={1} onPress={onClose}>
                <View style={[styles.modalCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]} onStartShouldSetResponder={() => true}>
                    <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Select Date</Text>

                    {/* Year Selector */}
                    <View style={styles.pickerRow}>
                        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setYear(y => y - 1)} style={[styles.stepBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}>
                            <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={18} color={colors.textPrimary} />
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                        <Text style={[styles.pickerValueText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{year}</Text>
                        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setYear(y => y + 1)} style={[styles.stepBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}>
                            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color={colors.textPrimary} />
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>

                    {/* Month Grid */}
                    <View style={styles.monthGrid}>
                        {months.map((m, idx) => (
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                key={m}
                                onPress={() => setMonth(idx)}
                                style={[
                                    styles.monthCell,
                                    { backgroundColor: month === idx ? colors.primary : colors.inputBg },
                                ]}
                            >
                                <Text style={[styles.monthCellText, { color: month === idx ? '#fff' : colors.textPrimary }]}>{m}</Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        ))}
                    </View>

                    {/* Day Selector */}
                    <View style={styles.pickerRow}>
                        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setDay(d => Math.max(1, d - 1))} style={[styles.stepBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}>
                            <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={18} color={colors.textPrimary} />
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                        <Text style={[styles.pickerValueText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Day {Math.min(day, daysInMonth)}</Text>
                        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setDay(d => Math.min(daysInMonth, d + 1))} style={[styles.stepBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}>
                            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color={colors.textPrimary} />
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>

                    {/* Modal Buttons */}
                    <View style={styles.modalBtnRow}>
                        <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={[styles.modalBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}>
                            <Text style={[styles.modalBtnText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Cancel</Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                        <TouchableOpacity onPress={handleConfirm} style={[styles.modalBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}>
                            <Text style={[styles.modalBtnText, { color: '#fff' }]}>Confirm</Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>
        </Modal>
    );
}

export default function CategoryTransactionsScreen() {
    const router = useRouter();
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const params = useLocalSearchParams();
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();

    const {
        transactions, currency, assets, exchangeRates,
        convertCurrency, deleteTransaction
    } = useApp();

    const rawCategoryParam = Array.isArray(params?.category) ? params.category[0] : params?.category;
    const initialPeriod = (Array.isArray(params?.period) ? params.period[0] : params?.period) || 'monthly';
    const initialRefDate = (Array.isArray(params?.refDate) ? params.refDate[0] : params?.refDate);

    const [period, setPeriod] = useState(initialPeriod);
    const [refDate, setRefDate] = useState(initialRefDate ? new Date(initialRefDate) : new Date());
    const [showDateModal, setShowDateModal] = useState(false);
    const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (params?.period) {
            const p = Array.isArray(params.period) ? params.period[0] : params.period;
            if (['daily', 'weekly', 'monthly', 'all'].includes(p)) {
                setPeriod(p);
            }
        }
    }, [params?.period]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (params?.refDate) {
            const r = Array.isArray(params.refDate) ? params.refDate[0] : params.refDate;
            const parsed = new Date(r);
            if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
                setRefDate(parsed);
            }
        }
    }, [params?.refDate]);

    // Resolved category details
    const categoryInfo = useMemo(() => {
        return getCategoryById(rawCategoryParam);
    }, [rawCategoryParam]);

    const targetCatId = useMemo(() => {
        return normalizeCategoryId(categoryInfo.id);
    }, [categoryInfo]);

    // Period date navigation
    const navigate = (direction) => {
        if (period === 'all') return;
        const fn = direction === 'next'
            ? (period === 'daily' ? addDays : period === 'weekly' ? addWeeks : addMonths)
            : (period === 'daily' ? subDays : period === 'weekly' ? subWeeks : subMonths);
        setRefDate(fn(refDate, 1));
    };

    // Filter transactions for active period
    const periodTransactions = useMemo(() => {
        return filterTransactionsByPeriod(transactions, period, refDate);
    }, [transactions, period, refDate]);

    // Overall spending for the period across all categories
    const totalPeriodSpending = useMemo(() => {
        return periodTransactions
            .filter(t => t.type === 'spending')
            .reduce((sum, t) => sum + Math.abs(convertCurrency(t.amount, t.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates)), 0);
    }, [periodTransactions, currency, exchangeRates, convertCurrency]);

    // Matching transactions for this category
    const categoryTransactions = useMemo(() => {
        return periodTransactions.filter(t => {
            if (t.type !== 'spending') return false;

            // Check if transaction has splits
            if (Array.isArray(t.splits) && t.splits.length > 0) {
                return t.splits.some(s => normalizeCategoryId(s.category) === targetCatId);
            }

            const catNormalized = normalizeCategoryId(t.category);
            if (catNormalized === targetCatId) return true;

            // Fallback for vehicle expenses if target is transport ('Car')
            if (targetCatId === 'transport' && t.vehicleId) return true;

            return false;
        }).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));
    }, [periodTransactions, targetCatId]);

    // Category summary statistics
    const stats = useMemo(() => {
        let total = 0;
        categoryTransactions.forEach(t => {
            if (Array.isArray(t.splits) && t.splits.length > 0) {
                const matchingSplits = t.splits.filter(s => normalizeCategoryId(s.category) === targetCatId);
                const splitSum = matchingSplits.reduce((acc, s) => acc + Math.abs(s.amount || 0), 0);
                total += convertCurrency(splitSum, t.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates);
            } else {
                total += Math.abs(convertCurrency(t.amount, t.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates));
            }
        });

        const count = categoryTransactions.length;
        const avg = count > 0 ? total / count : 0;
        const percentage = totalPeriodSpending > 0 ? (total / totalPeriodSpending) * 100 : 0;

        return { total, count, avg, percentage };
    }, [categoryTransactions, targetCatId, totalPeriodSpending, currency, exchangeRates, convertCurrency]);

    // Formatted period label for navigation bar
    const periodLabel = useMemo(() => {
        if (period === 'all') return 'All Time';
        if (period === 'daily') return format(refDate, 'MMM dd, yyyy');
        const { start, end } = getDateRange(period, refDate);
        return `${format(start, 'MMM dd, yyyy')} - ${format(end, 'MMM dd, yyyy')}`;
    }, [period, refDate]);

    // Confirm deletion
    const confirmDeleteTx = (tx) => {
        Alert.alert('Delete Transaction', 'Are you sure you want to delete this transaction?', [
            { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
            {
                text: 'Delete',
                style: 'destructive',
                onPress: () => deleteTransaction(tx.id),
            },
        ]);
    };

    return (
        <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: Platform.OS === 'android' ? insets.top : 0 }]}>
            {/* SVG Gradient Background */}
            <Svg
                key={colors.background}
                style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject}
                width={screenWidth}
                height={screenHeight}
                pointerEvents="none"
            >
                <Defs>
                    <SvgGrad id="bgGradCatTx" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                        <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                    </SvgGrad>
                </Defs>
                <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} fill="url(#bgGradCatTx)" />
            </Svg>

            {/* Modal Drag Handle */}
            <View style={[styles.handle, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />

            {/* ── Top Header ── */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => {
                        if (router.canDismiss?.()) {
                            router.dismiss();
                        } else {
                            router.back();
                        }
                    }}
                    style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                    activeOpacity={0.7}
                    accessibilityLabel="Back"
                    accessibilityRole="button"
                >
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <View style={styles.headerCenter}>
                    <View style={[styles.categoryHeaderBadge, { backgroundColor: categoryInfo.color }]}>
                        <Ionicons name={categoryInfo.icon} size={15} color="#fff" />
                    </View>
                    <Text style={[styles.headerTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {categoryInfo.name} Spending
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.headerSubtitle, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                        {stats.count} transaction{stats.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
                    </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.headerSpacer} />
            </View>

            {/* ── Period Selector Tabs ── */}
            <View style={styles.periodTabsWrap}>
                <View style={[styles.periodTabs, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                    {['daily', 'weekly', 'monthly', 'all'].map(p => {
                        const isActive = period === p;
                        return (
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                key={p}
                                style={[
                                    styles.periodTab,
                                    isActive && [styles.periodTabActive, { backgroundColor: colors.primary, shadowColor: colors.primary }],
                                ]}
                                onPress={() => { setPeriod(p); setRefDate(new Date()); }}
                                activeOpacity={0.75}
                            >
                                <Text
                                    style={[
                                        styles.periodText,
                                        { color: colors.textSecondary },
                                        isActive && styles.periodTextActive,
                                    ]}
                                >
                                    {p === 'all' ? 'All Time' : p.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + p.slice(1)}
                                </Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        );
                    })}
                </View>
            </View>

            {/* ── Date Navigator ── */}
            {period === 'all' ? (
                <View style={styles.allTimeBanner}>
                    <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={14} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                    <Text style={[styles.allTimeText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                        Showing all transactions
                    </Text>
                </View>
            ) : (
                <View style={styles.dateNav}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                        style={[styles.navArrow, { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]}
                        onPress={() => navigate('prev')}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                    >
                        <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={18} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>

                    <TouchableOpacity
                        style={[styles.dateLabelWrap, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}
                        onPress={() => setShowDateModal(true)}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                    >
                        <Text style={[styles.dateLabel, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{periodLabel}</Text>
                        <Ionicons name="calendar-outline" size={14} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>

                    <TouchableOpacity
                        style={[styles.navArrow, { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]}
                        onPress={() => navigate('next')}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                    >
                        <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
            )}

            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
                {/* ── Summary KPI Banner ── */}
                <View style={styles.summarySection}>
                    <View style={styles.summaryTopRow}>
                        <View>
                            <Text style={[styles.summaryLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Total {categoryInfo.name} Spent</Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.summaryAmount, { color: categoryInfo.color }]}>
                                {formatCurrency(stats.total, currency)}
                            </Text>
                        </View>
                        <View style={[styles.summaryIconCircle, { backgroundColor: `${categoryInfo.color}15` }]}>
                            <Ionicons name={categoryInfo.icon} size={28} color={categoryInfo.color} />
                        </View>
                    </View>

                    {/* Stats Metric Pills */}
                    <View style={styles.statsRow}>
                        <View style={[styles.statPill, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
                            <Text style={[styles.statPillVal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                                {stats.percentage.toFixed(1)}%
                            </Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.statPillLbl, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>of Spending</Text>
                        </View>
                        <View style={[styles.statPill, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
                            <Text style={[styles.statPillVal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                                {stats.count}
                            </Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.statPillLbl, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>Transactions</Text>
                        </View>
                        <View style={[styles.statPill, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
                            <Text style={[styles.statPillVal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                                {formatCurrency(stats.avg, currency)}
                            </Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.statPillLbl, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>Average / Tx</Text>
                        </View>
                    </View>
                </View>

                {/* ── Transaction List Section ── */}
                <View style={styles.listSection}>
                    <Text style={[styles.sectionHeading, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                        Transactions ({categoryTransactions.length})
                    </Text>

                    {categoryTransactions.length === 0 ? (
                        <View style={[styles.emptyCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
                            <View style={[styles.emptyIconWrap, { backgroundColor: `${categoryInfo.color}14` }]}>
                                <Ionicons name={categoryInfo.icon} size={36} color={categoryInfo.color} />
                            </View>
                            <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                                No {categoryInfo.name} Transactions
                            </Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.emptyBody, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                                There are no spending records for {categoryInfo.name} in {period === 'all' ? 'your all-time history' : `this ${period === 'monthly' ? 'month' : period === 'weekly' ? 'week' : 'day'}`}.
                            </Text>
                        </View>
                    ) : (
                        categoryTransactions.map(tx => {
                            const isExpanded = expandedId === tx.id;
                            const asset = (assets || []).find(a => a.id === tx.assetId);
                            const rawAmount = Math.abs(tx.amount || 0);
                            const convertedAmt = convertCurrency(rawAmount, tx.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates);
                            const isDiffCurrency = tx.currency && tx.currency !== currency;

                            const formattedDate = new Date(tx.date).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                                year: 'numeric',
                                month: 'short',
                                day: 'numeric',
                            });

                            return (
                                <TouchableOpacity
                                    key={tx.id}
                                    style={[styles.txCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}
                                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                                    onPress={() => setExpandedId(isExpanded ? null : tx.id)}
                                >
                                    <View style={styles.txMain}>
                                        <View style={[styles.txIconCircle, { backgroundColor: `${categoryInfo.color}18` }]}>
                                            <Ionicons
                                                name={tx.vehicleLogType === 'refuel' ? 'water-outline' : tx.vehicleLogType === 'maintenance' ? 'build-outline' : categoryInfo.icon}
                                                size={20}
                                                color={categoryInfo.color}
                                            />
                                        </View>

                                        <View style={styles.txInfoCol}>
                                            <Text style={[styles.txDesc, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                                {tx.description || categoryInfo.name}
                                            </Text>
                                            <View style={styles.txMetaRow}>
                                                <Text style={[styles.txDate, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                                                    {formattedDate} · {formatTime(tx.date)}
                                                </Text>
                                                {asset && (
                                                    <View style={[styles.assetPill, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.04)' }]}>
                                                        <Text style={[styles.assetPillText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{asset.name}</Text>
                                                    </View>
                                                )}
                                            </View>
                                        </View>

                                        <View style={styles.txAmountCol}>
                                            <Text style={[styles.txAmountText, { color: '#dc2626' }]}>
                                                -{formatCurrency(convertedAmt, currency)}
                                            </Text>
                                            {isDiffCurrency && (
                                                <Text style={[styles.txOrigAmountText, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>
                                                    ({formatCurrency(rawAmount, tx.currency)})
                                                </Text>
                                            )}
                                            <Ionicons
                                                name={isExpanded ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'}
                                                size={14}
                                                color={colors.textMuted}
                                                style={{ marginTop: 2 }}
                                            />
                                        </View>
                                    </View>

                                    {/* Expanded Details Section */}
                                    {isExpanded && (
                                        <View style={styles.txExpanded}>
                                            <View style={[styles.txDivider, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />

                                            {/* Vehicle Specific Info */}
                                            {tx.vehicleOdometer != null && (
                                                <View style={styles.detailRow}>
                                                    <Ionicons name="speedometer-outline" size={14} color={colors.primary} />
                                                    <Text style={[styles.detailLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Odometer:</Text>
                                                    <Text style={[styles.detailVal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{tx.vehicleOdometer.toLocaleString()} km</Text>
                                                </View>
                                            )}
                                            {tx.vehicleVolume != null && (
                                                <View style={styles.detailRow}>
                                                    <Ionicons name="water-outline" size={14} color="#059669" />
                                                    <Text style={[styles.detailLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Volume:</Text>
                                                    <Text style={[styles.detailVal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{tx.vehicleVolume} L</Text>
                                                </View>
                                            )}
                                            {tx.vehicleMaintenanceItems && (
                                                <View style={styles.detailRow}>
                                                    <Ionicons name="build-outline" size={14} color="#f59e0b" />
                                                    <Text style={[styles.detailLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Service:</Text>
                                                    <Text style={[styles.detailVal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{tx.vehicleMaintenanceItems}</Text>
                                                </View>
                                            )}

                                            {/* Notes */}
                                            {tx.notes ? (
                                                <View style={styles.detailRow}>
                                                    <Ionicons name="document-text-outline" size={14} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                                                    <Text style={[styles.detailLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Notes:</Text>
                                                    <Text style={[styles.detailVal, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{tx.notes}</Text>
                                                </View>
                                            ) : null}

                                            {/* Actions: Edit & Delete buttons matching Vehicle Dashboard sizing */}
                                            <View style={styles.txActions}>
                                                <TouchableOpacity
                                                    style={styles.editBtn}
                                                    onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/add-transaction', params: { editId: tx.id } })}
                                                    activeOpacity={0.75}
                                                >
                                                    <Ionicons name="pencil" size={14} color="#6A6FF8" />
                                                    <Text style={styles.editTxt}>Edit</Text>
                                                </TouchableOpacity>

                                                <TouchableOpacity
                                                    style={styles.deleteBtn}
                                                    onPress={() => confirmDeleteTx(tx)}
                                                    activeOpacity={0.75}
                                                >
                                                    <Ionicons name="trash" size={14} color="#dc2626" />
                                                    <Text style={styles.deleteTxt}>Delete</Text>
                                                </TouchableOpacity>
                                            </View>
                                        </View>
                                    )}
                                </TouchableOpacity>
                            );
                        })
                    )}
                </View>
                <View style={{ height: 100 }} />
            </ScrollView>

            <DatePickerModal
                visible={showDateModal}
                value={refDate}
                onConfirm={setRefDate}
                onClose={() => setShowDateModal(false)}
                colors={colors}
            />
        </View>
    );
}

================ FILE: app/chatbot.js ================

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
    Platform, View, Text, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity, TextInput, KeyboardAvoidingView, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, useWindowDimensions, Alert, Image, Modal, FlatList
} from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import { sendChatMessage } from '../services/aiChatbot';
import { extractTextFromDocumentUri } from '../services/localOCR';
import * as DocumentPicker from 'expo-document-picker';
import { Colors, Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { findMatchingAsset } from '../utils/helpers';

const SUGGESTIONS = [
    'Show my spending summary',
    'What did I spend most on?',
    'How much did I save this month?',
    'Am I on track with budget?',
    'Suggest ways to save more',
];

export default function Chatbot() {
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const router = useRouter();
    const { transactions, currency, savingsGoals, budgets, assets, vehicles, chatSessions, addChatSession, updateChatSession, deleteChatSession, exchangeRates } = useApp();
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();

    const welcomeMessage = {
        id: 'welcome',
        role: 'assistant',
        text: "Hi! I'm your AI financial assistant 👋\n\nI can help you analyze your spending, track savings goals, and give personalized financial insights. What would you like to know?\n\n*Note: All AI insights are for informational purposes only and do not constitute financial advice.*",
    };
    const [messages, setMessages] = useState([welcomeMessage]);
    const [currentSessionId, setCurrentSessionId] = useState(null);
    const [showHistory, setShowHistory] = useState(false);
    const [input, setInput] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [uploadingAttachment, setUploadingAttachment] = useState(false);
    const [attachments, setAttachments] = useState([]);
    const scrollRef = useRef(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        const timer = setTimeout(() => scrollRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true }), 100);
        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }, [messages]);

    const state = { transactions, budgets, assets: assets || [], vehicles: vehicles || [], currency, exchangeRates };

    const addImageAttachment = async ({ uri, name, mimeType }) => {
        if (!uri) return;
        const newAttachment = {
            id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7),
            uri,
            name: name || `Attachment-${attachments.length + 1}`,
            mimeType: mimeType || 'image/jpeg',
            text: null, // Text extraction is deferred until send
        };
        setAttachments(prev => [...prev, newAttachment]);
    };

    const pickPhotoAttachment = async () => {
        const permResult = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
        if (!permResult.granted) {
            Alert.alert('Permission needed', 'Please grant gallery permission to attach photos.');
            return;
        }

        const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
            mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
            quality: 1,
            base64: false,
            allowsEditing: false,
        });

        if (result.canceled || !result.assets?.length) return;
        const asset = result.assets[0];
        await addImageAttachment({
            uri: asset.uri,
            name: asset.fileName || 'Photo Attachment',
            mimeType: asset.mimeType || 'image/jpeg',
        });
    };

    const pickDocumentAttachment = async () => {
        try {
            const result = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({
                type: ['application/pdf', 'text/plain'],
                copyToCacheDirectory: true,
            });

            if (result.canceled || !result.assets?.length) return;
            const asset = result.assets[0];
            await addImageAttachment({
                uri: asset.uri,
                name: asset.name || 'Document Attachment',
                mimeType: asset.mimeType || 'application/pdf',
            });
        } catch (err) {
            Alert.alert('Attachment Error', 'Could not pick document.');
        }
    };

    const renderMarkdownText = (text, baseStyle) => {
        if (!text) return null;
        // Strip <think> tags and their contents (even if unclosed during streaming)
        const cleanedText = text.replace(/<think>[\s\S]*?(?:<\/think>|$)/gi, '').trim();
        const parts = cleanedText.split(/(\*\*.*?\*\*)/g);
        return (
            <Text style={baseStyle}>
                {parts.map((part, i) => {
                    if (part.startsWith('**') && part.endsWith('**')) {
                        return <Text key={i} style={{ fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>{part.slice(2, -2)}</Text>;
                    }
                    return part;
                })}
            </Text>
        );
    };

    const handleAttachPress = () => {
        Alert.alert('Attach', 'Choose what to add to the chat', [
            { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
            { text: 'Photo', onPress: pickPhotoAttachment },
            { text: 'Document', onPress: pickDocumentAttachment },
        ]);
    };

    const removeAttachment = (attachmentId) => {
        setAttachments(prev => prev.filter(item => item.id !== attachmentId));
    };

    const send = async (text, forceSkipTxCheck = false, injectedAttachments = null) => {
        const typedText = (text || input).trim();
        const attachmentsToSend = injectedAttachments || [...attachments];
        let userText = typedText || (attachmentsToSend.length > 0
            ? 'Please analyze the attached files/photos and explain the key financial insights.'
            : '');
        if (!userText || loading || uploadingAttachment) return;

        const isTransactionMode = attachmentsToSend.some(att => att.mimeType === 'application/vnd.financeflow.transaction');

        if (!isTransactionMode && !forceSkipTxCheck) {
            const txKeywords = /\b(bought|spend|spent|paid|cost|deposited|earned|salary|purchase|purchased|received|pay|income|expense|sold)\b/i;
            if (txKeywords.test(userText)) {
                Alert.alert(
                    "Add as Transaction?",
                    "It looks like you might be describing a transaction. Do you want to record this?",
                    [
                        {
                            text: "No, just chat",
                            onPress: () => send(userText, true, attachmentsToSend),
                            style: 'cancel'
                        },
                        {
                            text: "Yes, add transaction",
                            onPress: () => {
                                const txAtt = {
                                    id: 'tx-' + Date.now(),
                                    name: 'Transaction Context',
                                    mimeType: 'application/vnd.financeflow.transaction',
                                    text: 'User is providing transaction details.'
                                };
                                send(userText, true, [...attachmentsToSend, txAtt]);
                            }
                        }
                    ]
                );
                return; // Stop the send, wait for user's alert choice
            }
        }

        let finalUserText = userText;
        if (isTransactionMode) {
            const today = new Date();
            const tzOffset = today.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;
            const localToday = new Date(today - tzOffset).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
            const yesterday = new Date(today.getTime() - 86400000);
            const localYesterday = new Date(yesterday - tzOffset).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

            const assetsStr = (assets || []).map(a => `${a.name} (ID: ${a.id})`).join(', ');

            //PROMPT FOR ADD TRANSACTION ON AI ASSISTANT
            finalUserText = `I want to add a transaction. Please extract the transaction details from my message and return a strict JSON object (enclosed in \`\`\`json and \`\`\`) with these keys:
- type: "spending" or "income" (or "move" if transferring between accounts)
- amount: the total amount as a positive number
- category: ONLY the exact ID string (e.g. "food", "transport", "salary") from the Available Categories list (or leave empty string if unsure)
- description: a short description of the transaction
- items: array of objects with { name, quantity, price } if specific items are mentioned, otherwise empty array
- assetId: ONLY the exact ID string from the following list if I mention receiving/paying from an account/asset: [${assetsStr}]. You must match the account name exactly to the ID!
- date: ISO 8601 date string for when the transaction happened. (Today is ${localToday}, yesterday was ${localYesterday}. If I say yesterday, use ${localYesterday} exactly!)

My message: ${userText}`;
        }
        setInput('');
        setAttachments([]);
        const userMsg = { id: String(Date.now()), role: 'user', text: userText, attachments: attachmentsToSend };
        const assistantId = String(Date.now() + 1);
        let newMessages = [...messages, userMsg, { id: assistantId, role: 'assistant', text: '' }];
        setMessages(newMessages);
        const chatHistory = [...messages]
            .filter(m => m.id !== 'welcome')
            .map(m => ({ role: m.role, content: m.text }));

        // setMessages already done above
        setLoading(true);
        let activeSessionId = currentSessionId;
        if (!activeSessionId) {
            activeSessionId = await addChatSession({
                title: (isTransactionMode ? 'Add Transaction' : userText).substring(0, 30) + ((isTransactionMode ? 'Add Transaction' : userText).length > 30 ? '...' : ''),
                messages: newMessages
            });
            setCurrentSessionId(activeSessionId);
        } else {
            await updateChatSession(activeSessionId, newMessages);
        }

        try {
            for (const att of attachmentsToSend) {
                if (!att.text) {
                    setMessages(prev => prev.map(m =>
                        m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, text: `Reading ${att.name}... Might take a minute or two` } : m
                    ));
                    try {
                        att.text = await extractTextFromDocumentUri(att.uri);
                    } catch (e) {
                        att.text = `[Failed to read: ${e.message}]`;
                    }
                }
            }
            setMessages(prev => prev.map(m =>
                m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, text: '' } : m
            ));

            let streamedText = '';
            const aiResponse = await sendChatMessage(
                finalUserText,
                state,
                chatHistory,
                (token) => {
                    streamedText += token;
                    setMessages(prev =>
                        prev.map(m => m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, text: streamedText } : m)
                    );
                },
                attachmentsToSend,
                isTransactionMode
            );
            const finalText = streamedText || aiResponse?.text || '';
            setMessages(prev => {
                const updated = prev.map(m => m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, text: finalText } : m);
                updateChatSession(activeSessionId, updated).catch(console.error);
                return updated;
            });
        } catch (err) {
            setMessages(prev => {
                const updated = prev.map(m => m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, text: '⚠️ ' + (err.message || 'Could not get response.') } : m);
                updateChatSession(activeSessionId, updated).catch(console.error);
                return updated;
            });
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };


    return (
        <KeyboardAvoidingView style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]} behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}>
            {/* Gradient background */}
            <Svg key={colors.background} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject} width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} pointerEvents="none">
                <Defs>
                    <SvgGrad id="bgGrad" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                        <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="0.4" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                    </SvgGrad>
                </Defs>
                <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} fill="url(#bgGrad)" />
            </Svg>

            {/* Header */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => { if (router.canDismiss?.()) { router.dismiss(); } else { router.back(); } }} style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}>
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <View style={styles.headerCenter}>
                    <View style={[styles.aiAvatarSmall, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="sparkles" size={16} color="#fff" />
                    </View>
                    <View>
                        <Text style={[styles.headerTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>AI Assistant</Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.headerSub, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{loading ? 'Thinking...' : 'Ready to help'}</Text>
                    </View>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.headerRight}>
                    <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => { setMessages([welcomeMessage]); setCurrentSessionId(null); }} style={[styles.headerIconBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="add" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setShowHistory(true)} style={[styles.headerIconBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
            </View>

            {/* Messages */}
            <ScrollView
                ref={scrollRef}
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
                contentContainerStyle={styles.msgList}
                keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            >
                {messages.map((msg) => (
                    <View key={msg.id} style={[styles.msgRow, msg.role === 'user' ? styles.msgRowUser : styles.msgRowAI]}>
                        {msg.role === 'assistant' && (
                            <View style={[styles.aiAvatar, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}>
                                <Ionicons name="sparkles" size={14} color="#fff" />
                            </View>
                        )}
                        <View style={[styles.bubble, msg.role === 'user' ? [styles.bubbleUser, { backgroundColor: colors.primary, shadowColor: colors.primary }] : [styles.bubbleAI, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, shadowColor: isDark ? '#000' : '#1A1040' }]]}>
                            {msg.attachments && msg.attachments.length > 0 && (
                                <View style={styles.msgAttachments}>
                                    {msg.attachments.map((att, idx) => (
                                        att.mimeType?.startsWith('image/') ? (
                                            <Image key={idx} source={{ uri: att.uri }} style={styles.msgImage} />
                                        ) : (
                                            <View key={idx} style={styles.msgDoc}>
                                                <Ionicons name="document-text" size={16} color="#fff" />
                                                <Text style={styles.msgDocText} numberOfLines={1}>{att.name}</Text>
                                            </View>
                                        )
                                    ))}
                                </View>
                            )}
                            {msg.role === 'assistant' && !msg.text && loading ? (
                                <View style={styles.typingDots}>
                                    <View style={[styles.dot, styles.dot1, { backgroundColor: colors.textMuted }]} />
                                    <View style={[styles.dot, styles.dot2, { backgroundColor: colors.textMuted }]} />
                                    <View style={[styles.dot, styles.dot3, { backgroundColor: colors.textMuted }]} />
                                </View>
                            ) : (
                                msg.role === 'assistant' && msg.text.includes('```json') ? (
                                    <View>
                                        <Text style={[styles.bubbleAIText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                                            {msg.text.replace(/```json\n[\s\S]*?\n```/, 'I have extracted the transaction details for you.')}
                                        </Text>
                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                            style={[styles.txAddBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}
                                            onPress={() => {
                                                const match = msg.text.match(/```json\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/);
                                                if (match) {
                                                    try {
                                                        const txData = JSON.parse(match[1]);
                                                        if (assets && assets.length > 0) {
                                                            const rawAsset = txData.assetId || txData.accountId || txData.account || txData.asset || txData.assetName || txData.payFrom;
                                                            if (rawAsset) {
                                                                const matched = findMatchingAsset(rawAsset, assets);
                                                                if (matched) {
                                                                    txData.assetId = matched.id;
                                                                }
                                                            }
                                                            const rawFrom = txData.fromAssetId || txData.fromAccount || txData.from;
                                                            if (rawFrom) {
                                                                const matchedFrom = findMatchingAsset(rawFrom, assets);
                                                                if (matchedFrom) {
                                                                    txData.fromAssetId = matchedFrom.id;
                                                                }
                                                            }
                                                            const rawTo = txData.toAssetId || txData.toAccount || txData.to;
                                                            if (rawTo) {
                                                                const matchedTo = findMatchingAsset(rawTo, assets);
                                                                if (matchedTo) {
                                                                    txData.toAssetId = matchedTo.id;
                                                                }
                                                            }
                                                        }
                                                        // Use setTimeout to avoid iOS touch registry crash
                                                        setTimeout(() => {
                                                            router.push({ pathname: '/add-transaction', params: { prefill: JSON.stringify(txData) } });
                                                        }, 10);
                                                    } catch (e) {
                                                        Alert.alert('Error', 'Could not parse transaction data.');
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }}
                                            activeOpacity={0.8}
                                        >
                                            <Ionicons name="add-circle-outline" size={18} color="#fff" />
                                            <Text style={styles.txAddBtnTxt}>Add to transaction</Text>
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    </View>
                                ) : (
                                    msg.role === 'user' ? (
                                        <Text style={styles.bubbleUserText}>{msg.text}</Text>
                                    ) : (
                                        renderMarkdownText(msg.text, [styles.bubbleAIText, { color: colors.textPrimary }])
                                    )
                                )
                            )}
                            {msg.role === 'assistant' && !!msg.text && (
                                <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: 6 }}>
                                    ℹ️ AI-generated content · Informational only, not financial advice.
                                </Text>
                            )}
                        </View>
                    </View>
                ))}
            </ScrollView>

            {/* Suggestions */}
            {messages.length === 1 && !loading && (
                <View style={styles.suggestionsWrap}>
                    <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.suggestionsRow}>
                        {/* First Item: Add transaction */}
                        <TouchableOpacity
                            style={[styles.suggestionChip, { backgroundColor: colors.primary, borderColor: colors.primary, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: Spacing.xs }]}
                            onPress={() => {
                                setAttachments(prev => {
                                    if (prev.some(a => a.mimeType === 'application/vnd.financeflow.transaction')) {
                                        return prev; // Prevent adding multiple times
                                    }
                                    return [...prev, {
                                        id: 'tx-' + Date.now(),
                                        name: 'Transaction Context',
                                        mimeType: 'application/vnd.financeflow.transaction',
                                        text: 'User is providing transaction details.'
                                    }];
                                });
                            }}
                            activeOpacity={0.8}
                        >
                            <Ionicons name="add" size={16} color="#ffffff" />
                            <Text style={[styles.suggestionTxt, { color: '#ffffff' }]}>Add transaction</Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>

                        {/* Second Item: Show my spending summary */}
                        <TouchableOpacity
                            style={[styles.suggestionChip, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.border }]}
                            onPress={() => send('Show my spending summary')}
                            activeOpacity={0.8}
                        >
                            <Text style={[styles.suggestionTxt, { color: colors.primary }]}>Show my spending summary</Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>

                        {/* Remaining suggestions */}
                        {SUGGESTIONS.filter(s => s !== 'Show my spending summary').map(s => (
                            <TouchableOpacity key={s} style={[styles.suggestionChip, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.border }]} onPress={() => send(s)} activeOpacity={0.8}>
                                <Text style={[styles.suggestionTxt, { color: colors.primary }]}>{s}</Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        ))}
                    </ScrollView>
                </View>
            )}

            {/* Input */}
            <View style={styles.inputArea}>
                {attachments.length > 0 && (
                    <ScrollView
                        horizontal
                        showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
                        contentContainerStyle={styles.attachmentRow}
                    >
                        {attachments.map((attachment) => (
                            <View key={attachment.id} style={[styles.attachmentChip, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                                <Ionicons name={attachment.mimeType === 'application/vnd.financeflow.transaction' ? "card-outline" : attachment.mimeType?.startsWith('image/') ? "image-outline" : "document-text-outline"} size={14} color={colors.primary} />
                                <Text style={[styles.attachmentText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{attachment.name}</Text>
                                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => removeAttachment(attachment.id)}>
                                    <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={16} color={colors.textMuted} />
                                </TouchableOpacity>
                            </View>
                        ))}
                    </ScrollView>
                )}

                <View style={[styles.inputBox, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.inputBorder, shadowColor: isDark ? '#000' : '#1A1040' }]}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                        style={[styles.attachBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }, (loading || uploadingAttachment) && styles.attachBtnDisabled]}
                        onPress={handleAttachPress}
                        disabled={loading || uploadingAttachment}
                    >
                        {uploadingAttachment ? (
                            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colors.primary} />
                        ) : (
                            <Ionicons name="add" size={24} color={colors.primary} />
                        )}
                    </TouchableOpacity>

                    <TextInput
                        style={[styles.textInput, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}
                        placeholder="Ask about your finances..."
                        placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                        value={input}
                        onChangeText={setInput}
                        multiline
                        maxLength={1000}
                        returnKeyType="send"
                        onSubmitEditing={() => !loading && send()}
                    />
                    <TouchableOpacity
                        style={[styles.sendBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary, shadowColor: colors.primary }, ((!input.trim() && attachments.length === 0) || loading || uploadingAttachment) && [styles.sendBtnDisabled, { backgroundColor: colors.card, shadowColor: 'transparent' }]]}
                        onPress={() => send()}
                        disabled={(!input.trim() && attachments.length === 0) || loading || uploadingAttachment}
                        activeOpacity={0.85}
                    >
                        {loading ? (
                            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#fff" />
                        ) : (
                            <Ionicons name="arrow-up" size={18} color="#fff" />
                        )}
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
                <Text style={[styles.aiDisclaimer, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>AI responses are informational only. Not financial advice.</Text>
            </View>
            {/* History Modal */}
            <Modal visible={showHistory} animationType="slide" transparent={true} onRequestClose={() => setShowHistory(false)}>
                <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
                    <View style={[styles.modalContent, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
                        <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
                            <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Chat History</Text>
                            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => requestAnimationFrame(() => setShowHistory(false))}>
                                <Ionicons name="close" size={24} color={colors.textPrimary} />
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>
                        {chatSessions.length === 0 ? (
                            <View style={styles.emptyHistory}>
                                <Ionicons name="chatbubbles-outline" size={48} color={colors.textMuted} />
                                <Text style={[styles.emptyHistoryText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>No previous chats found.</Text>
                            </View>
                        ) : (
                            <FlatList
                                data={chatSessions}
                                keyExtractor={item => item.id}
                                renderItem={({ item }) => (
                                    <TouchableOpacity
                                        style={[styles.historyItem, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: currentSessionId === item.id ? colors.primary : colors.border }]}
                                        onPress={() => {
                                            setMessages(item.messages);
                                            setCurrentSessionId(item.id);
                                            setShowHistory(false);
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                                            <Text style={[styles.historyTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{item.title}</Text>
                                            <Text style={[styles.historyDate, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{new Date(item.updatedAt).toLocaleString()}</Text>
                                        </View>
                                        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => {
                                            Alert.alert('Delete Chat', 'Are you sure you want to delete this chat?', [
                                                { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
                                                {
                                                    text: 'Delete', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => {
                                                        deleteChatSession(item.id);
                                                        if (currentSessionId === item.id) {
                                                            setMessages([welcomeMessage]);
                                                            setCurrentSessionId(null);
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            ]);
                                        }}>
                                            <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={20} color={colors.rose} />
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    </TouchableOpacity>
                                )}
                                contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: Spacing.xl }}
                            />
                        )}
                    </View>
                </View>
            </Modal>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#F0EEFF' },

    header: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg,
        paddingTop: 60,
        paddingBottom: Spacing.md,
    },
    backBtn: {
        width: 40,
        height: 40,
        borderRadius: 14,
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.1)',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
    },
    headerCenter: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: Spacing.sm,
    },
    aiAvatarSmall: {
        width: 32,

        borderRadius: 10,

        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',

        width: 28,

        flexShrink: 0,

    bubble: {
        maxWidth: '78%',

    },
    bubbleUser: {

        shadowOpacity: 0.25,
        shadowRadius: 8,

    bubbleAI: {

        borderBottomLeftRadius: 4,

        shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 },

    },
    bubbleUserText: {

    },
    bubbleAIText: {
        color: '#1A1040',
        fontSize: FontSize.md,

    },
    msgAttachments: {
        marginBottom: 8,
        gap: 8,
    },

        width: 140,

        borderRadius: 10,
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)',
    },
    msgDoc: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: 6,
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)',
        padding: 8,
        borderRadius: 8,
    },
    msgDocText: {
        color: '#fff',
        fontSize: FontSize.sm,
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium',
        flex: 1,
    },

    typingDots: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: 5,
        paddingVertical: 4,
    },
    dot: {
        width: 8,
        height: 8,
        borderRadius: 4,
        backgroundColor: '#8B7CC8',
    },
    dot1: { opacity: 1 },
    dot2: { opacity: 0.6 },
    dot3: { opacity: 0.3 },

    suggestionsWrap: { paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg, marginBottom: Spacing.sm },
    suggestionsRow: { gap: Spacing.sm },
    suggestionChip: {
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.08)',
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.md,
        paddingVertical: Spacing.sm,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.full,
        borderWidth: 1,
        borderColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.15)',
    },
    suggestionTxt: {
        fontSize: FontSize.sm,
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium',
        color: '#ff6600',
    },

    inputArea: {
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg,
        paddingBottom: Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 34 : 16,
        paddingTop: Spacing.sm,
    },
    inputBox: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: Spacing.sm,
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.92)',
        borderRadius: 22,
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.md,
        paddingTop: 8,
        paddingBottom: 8,
        borderWidth: 1,
        borderColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.1)',
        shadowColor: '#1A1040',
        shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 },
        shadowOpacity: 0.06,
        shadowRadius: 8,
        elevation: Platform.OS === 'android' ? 0 : 2,
    },
    attachBtn: {
        width: 34,
        height: 34,
        borderRadius: 12,
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.08)',
        borderWidth: 1,
        borderColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.16)',
    },
    attachBtnDisabled: {
        opacity: 0.55,
    },
    textInput: {
        flex: 1,
        fontSize: FontSize.md,
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular',
