================ FILE: app/custom-model-quantization.js ================

/**
 * custom-model-quantization.js
 *
 * After selecting a HuggingFace GGUF repo, this page shows available
 * quantization variants and lets the user pick one to download.
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
    Platform, View, Text, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity,
    ActivityIndicator, Alert, StyleSheet, useWindowDimensions,
} from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { saveCustomModel, getAllModels } from '../services/localAIConfig';
import { setActiveModelId, downloadModel, checkModelStatus } from '../services/localAI';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';

const HF_API = 'https://huggingface.co/api/models';

// Files that should NEVER be offered for download (incompatible / projector files)
const EXCLUDED_PATTERNS = [
    /mmproj/i,
    /\bBF16\b/i,
    /\bF32\b/i,
    /\bF16\b/i,
    /\.mmproj/i,
    /vision/i,
    /projector/i,
    /text-embedding/i,
    /tokenizer/i,
];

function isExcluded(filename) {
    return EXCLUDED_PATTERNS.some(re => re.test(filename));
}

function formatBytes(bytes) {
    if (!bytes || bytes <= 0) return 'Unknown size';
    if (bytes >= 1_073_741_824) return `${(bytes / 1_073_741_824).toFixed(2)} GB`;
    if (bytes >= 1_048_576) return `${(bytes / 1_048_576).toFixed(0)} MB`;
    return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`;
}

function extractQuantLabel(filename) {
    // e.g. model-Q4_K_M.gguf → Q4_K_M
    const match = filename.match(/[-._ ]((?:IQ|Q|q)\d[^\s.]*?)\.gguf$/i);
    return match ? match[1].toUpperCase() : filename.replace('.gguf', '');
}

export default function CustomModelQuantization() {
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const router = useRouter();
    const { repoId, modelName } = useLocalSearchParams();
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();

    const [files, setFiles] = useState([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState('');
    const [downloading, setDownloading] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!repoId) return;
        (async () => {
            setLoading(true);
            setError('');
            try {
                const url = `${HF_API}/${repoId}`;
                const response = await fetch(url, {
                    headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' },
                });
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`API returned ${response.status}`);

                const data = await response.json();
                const siblings = data.siblings || [];

                // Filter to only .gguf files, excluding incompatible ones
                const ggufFiles = siblings
                    .filter(s => s.rfilename && s.rfilename.endsWith('.gguf') && !isExcluded(s.rfilename))
                    .map(s => ({
                        filename: s.rfilename,
                        quantLabel: extractQuantLabel(s.rfilename),
                        size: s.size || null, // may be null
                    }));

                // Try to get file sizes if not present via a second call with blobs
                if (ggufFiles.some(f => !f.size)) {
                    try {
                        const blobUrl = `${HF_API}/${repoId}?blobs=true`;
                        const blobResp = await fetch(blobUrl, {
                            headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' },
                        });
                        if (blobResp.ok) {
                            const blobData = await blobResp.json();
                            const blobSiblings = blobData.siblings || [];
                            for (const f of ggufFiles) {
                                if (!f.size) {
                                    const match = blobSiblings.find(bs => bs.rfilename === f.filename);
                                    if (match?.size) f.size = match.size;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    } catch {
                        // Sizes remain unknown — that's fine
                    }
                }

                // Sort by size ascending (smallest first)
                ggufFiles.sort((a, b) => (a.size || Infinity) - (b.size || Infinity));
                setFiles(ggufFiles);
            } catch (err) {
                setError(err.message || 'Failed to load model files');
            } finally {
                setLoading(false);
            }
        })();
    }, [repoId]);

    const handleSelectQuant = useCallback(async (file) => {
        // Check if already added
        const existing = getAllModels().find(m => m.fileName === file.filename);
        if (existing) {
            Alert.alert('Already Added', 'This model variant is already in your model list.');
            return;
        }

        setDownloading(true);
        try {
            const customId = `custom-${repoId.replace(/\//g, '-')}-${file.quantLabel}`.toLowerCase();
            const downloadUrl = `https://huggingface.co/${repoId}/resolve/main/${file.filename}`;

            const modelConfig = {
                id: customId,
                name: `${modelName || repoId.split('/').pop()} ${file.quantLabel}`,
                description: `Custom model from ${repoId} (${file.quantLabel})`,
                fileName: file.filename,
                downloadUrl,
                sizeBytes: file.size || 0,
                sizeLabel: file.size ? `~${formatBytes(file.size)}` : 'Unknown size',
                contextLength: 4096,
                minContextLength: 1024,
                maxContextLength: 32768,
                gpuLayers: 0,
                forceOcrPipeline: false,
                recommendedForChat: true,
                recommendedForReceipt: false,
                recommendedForPdf: true,
                canThink: false,
                isCustom: true,
                hfRepoId: repoId,
            };

            await saveCustomModel(modelConfig);
            await setActiveModelId(customId);

            // Start download
            downloadModel(customId).catch(err => {
                console.warn('Custom model download error:', err?.message);
            });

            await checkModelStatus();

            // Navigate back to settings
            router.dismiss(2); // Go back 2 screens (quantization + search)
        } catch (err) {
            Alert.alert('Error', err.message || 'Failed to add model');
        } finally {
            setDownloading(false);
        }
    }, [repoId, modelName, router]);

    return (
        <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
            <Svg key={colors.background} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject} width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} pointerEvents="none">
                <Defs>
                    <SvgGrad id="bgGrad" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                        <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                    </SvgGrad>
                </Defs>
                <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} fill="url(#bgGrad)" />
            </Svg>

            {/* Header */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => router.back()} style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}>
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>Select Quantization</Text>
                <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
            </View>

            {/* Model info banner */}
            <View style={[styles.infoBanner, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                <View style={[styles.bannerIcon, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,102,0,0.15)' : 'rgba(255,102,0,0.08)' }]}>
                    <Ionicons name="cube" size={22} color={colors.primary} />
                </View>
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <Text style={[styles.bannerName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {modelName || repoId}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.bannerRepo, { color: colors.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {repoId}
                    </Text>
                </View>
            </View>

            {/* Content */}
            <ScrollView
                style={styles.scrollView}
                contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
            >
                {loading && (
                    <View style={styles.centerWrap}>
                        <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
                        <Text style={[styles.statusText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Loading quantization options…</Text>
                    </View>
                )}

                {error ? (
                    <View style={[styles.errorBox, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,122,135,0.1)' : 'rgba(255,122,135,0.06)', borderColor: colors.rose }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="alert-circle" size={16} color={colors.rose} />
                        <Text style={[styles.errorText, { color: colors.rose }]}>{error}</Text>
                    </View>
                ) : null}

                {!loading && files.length === 0 && !error && (
                    <View style={styles.centerWrap}>
                        <Ionicons name="warning-outline" size={40} color={colors.gold} />
                        <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>No compatible GGUF quantizations found in this repository.</Text>
                    </View>
                )}

                {downloading && (
                    <View style={[styles.downloadBanner, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,102,0,0.12)' : 'rgba(255,102,0,0.06)', borderColor: colors.primary }]}>
                        <ActivityIndicator color={colors.primary} />
                        <Text style={[styles.downloadBannerText, { color: colors.primary }]}>Adding model and starting download…</Text>
                    </View>
                )}

                {!loading && files.map((file, idx) => {
                    // Determine quant quality tier for visual hint
                    const ql = file.quantLabel.toUpperCase();
                    let tierColor = colors.textMuted;
                    let tierLabel = 'Standard';
                    if (/Q[2-3]|IQ[1-2]/.test(ql)) { tierColor = colors.gold; tierLabel = 'Lightweight'; }
                    else if (/Q4|IQ3|IQ4/.test(ql)) { tierColor = colors.emerald; tierLabel = 'Balanced'; }
                    else if (/Q[5-6]/.test(ql)) { tierColor = colors.blue; tierLabel = 'High Quality'; }
                    else if (/Q8|Q[7-9]/.test(ql)) { tierColor = colors.primary; tierLabel = 'Maximum'; }

                    return (
                        <TouchableOpacity
                            key={`${file.filename}-${idx}`}
                            style={[styles.quantCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}
                            onPress={() => handleSelectQuant(file)}
                            activeOpacity={0.82}
                            disabled={downloading}
                        >
                            <View style={styles.quantHeader}>
                                <View style={[styles.quantIconWrap, { backgroundColor: `${tierColor}15` }]}>
                                    <Ionicons name="layers" size={18} color={tierColor} />
                                </View>
                                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                                    <Text style={[styles.quantLabel, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{file.quantLabel}</Text>
                                    <Text style={[styles.quantFilename, { color: colors.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{file.filename}</Text>
                                </View>
                            </View>
                            <View style={styles.quantMeta}>
                                <View style={[styles.tierBadge, { backgroundColor: `${tierColor}12` }]}>
                                    <Text style={[styles.tierText, { color: tierColor }]}>{tierLabel}</Text>
                                </View>
                                <View style={[styles.sizeBadge, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.04)' }]}>
                                    <Ionicons name="cloud-download-outline" size={12} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                                    <Text style={[styles.sizeText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{formatBytes(file.size)}</Text>
                                </View>
                            </View>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                })}

                <View style={{ height: 40 }} />
            </ScrollView>
        </View>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1 },
    header: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg, paddingTop: 60, paddingBottom: Spacing.sm,
    },
    backBtn: {
        width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 14,
        alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
    },
    title: { fontSize: FontSize.xl, fontFamily: 'Poppins_800ExtraBold', flex: 1, textAlign: 'center' },
    infoBanner: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: Spacing.md,
        marginHorizontal: Spacing.lg, padding: Spacing.md,
        borderRadius: 16, borderWidth: 1, marginBottom: Spacing.sm,
    },
    bannerIcon: {

================ FILE: app/custom-model-search.js ================

/**
 * custom-model-search.js
 *
 * Search Hugging Face for GGUF models.
 * Displays results with download counts, lets user pick one to view quantizations.
 */

import React, { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
    Platform, View, Text, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity,
    TextInput, ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, useWindowDimensions,
} from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';

const HF_API = 'https://huggingface.co/api/models';

function formatDownloads(n) {
    if (!n || n < 0) return '0';
    if (n >= 1_000_000) return `${(n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1)}M`;
    if (n >= 1_000) return `${(n / 1_000).toFixed(1)}K`;
    return String(n);
}

export default function CustomModelSearch() {
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const router = useRouter();
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();

    const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    const [results, setResults] = useState([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState('');
    const debounceRef = useRef(null);

    const searchModels = useCallback(async (searchText) => {
        const trimmed = (searchText || '').trim();
        if (trimmed.length < 2) {
            setResults([]);
            setSearched(false);
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        setError('');
        setSearched(true);

        try {
            const url = `${HF_API}?filter=gguf&search=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}&sort=downloads&direction=-1&limit=25`;
            const response = await fetch(url, {
                headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' },
            });

            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(`HuggingFace API returned ${response.status}`);
            }

            const data = await response.json();

            // The filter=gguf parameter already ensures these are GGUF repos.
            // Siblings (file list) are NOT included in search results — they're
            // only available when fetching a single model's details, which happens
            // on the quantization page. So we use results directly.
            setResults(data);
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.message || 'Failed to search models');
            setResults([]);
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    }, []);

    const handleQueryChange = useCallback((text) => {
        setQuery(text);
        if (debounceRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
        debounceRef.current = setTimeout(() => searchModels(text), 500);
    }, [searchModels]);

    const handleSelect = useCallback((model) => {
        router.push({
            pathname: '/custom-model-quantization',
            params: { repoId: model.id, modelName: model.id.split('/').pop() || model.id },
        });
    }, [router]);

    return (
        <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
            <Svg key={colors.background} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject} width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} pointerEvents="none">
                <Defs>
                    <SvgGrad id="bgGrad" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                        <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                    </SvgGrad>
                </Defs>
                <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} fill="url(#bgGrad)" />
            </Svg>

            {/* Header */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => router.back()} style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}>
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Add Custom Model</Text>
                <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
            </View>

            {/* Search Bar */}
            <View style={[styles.searchWrap, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                <Ionicons name="search" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} />
                <TextInput
                    style={[styles.searchInput, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}
                    placeholder="Search GGUF models on Hugging Face…"
                    placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                    value={query}
                    onChangeText={handleQueryChange}
                    autoCapitalize="none"
                    autoCorrect={false}
                    returnKeyType="search"
                    onSubmitEditing={() => searchModels(query)}
                />
                {query.length > 0 && (
                    <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => { setQuery(''); setResults([]); setSearched(false); }}>
                        <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={18} color={colors.textMuted} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
            </View>

            {/* Results */}
            <ScrollView
                style={styles.scrollView}
                contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
                keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
            >
                {loading && (
                    <View style={styles.centerWrap}>
                        <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
                        <Text style={[styles.statusText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Searching Hugging Face…</Text>
                    </View>
                )}

                {error ? (
                    <View style={[styles.errorBox, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,122,135,0.1)' : 'rgba(255,122,135,0.06)', borderColor: colors.rose }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="alert-circle" size={16} color={colors.rose} />
                        <Text style={[styles.errorText, { color: colors.rose }]}>{error}</Text>
                    </View>
                ) : null}

                {!loading && searched && results.length === 0 && !error && (
                    <View style={styles.centerWrap}>
                        <Ionicons name="search-outline" size={40} color={colors.textMuted} />
                        <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>No GGUF models found for "{query}"</Text>
                    </View>
                )}

                {!loading && !searched && (
                    <View style={styles.centerWrap}>
                        <Ionicons name="sparkles-outline" size={48} color={colors.primary} style={{ opacity: 0.5 }} />
                        <Text style={[styles.hintTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Search for AI Models</Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.hintText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                            Search for GGUF models on Hugging Face.{'\n'}Try "Qwen", "Llama", "Gemma", or "Phi".
                        </Text>
                    </View>
                )}

                {results.map((model) => {
                    return (
                        <TouchableOpacity
                            key={model.id}
                            style={[styles.resultCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}
                            onPress={() => handleSelect(model)}
                            activeOpacity={0.82}
                        >
                            <View style={styles.resultHeader}>
                                <View style={[styles.modelIconWrap, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(255,102,0,0.15)' : 'rgba(255,102,0,0.08)' }]}>
                                    <Ionicons name="cube-outline" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                                </View>
                                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                                    <Text style={[styles.resultName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                        {model.id.split('/').pop()}
                                    </Text>
                                    <Text style={[styles.resultAuthor, { color: colors.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                        {model.author || model.id.split('/')[0]}
                                    </Text>
                                </View>
                                <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={16} color={colors.textMuted} />
                            </View>

                            <View style={styles.resultMeta}>
                                <View style={[styles.metaBadge, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(30,201,142,0.12)' : 'rgba(30,201,142,0.08)' }]}>
                                    <Ionicons name="download-outline" size={12} color={colors.emerald} />
                                    <Text style={[styles.metaText, { color: colors.emerald }]}>{formatDownloads(model.downloads)}</Text>
                                </View>
                                <View style={[styles.metaBadge, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(106,111,248,0.12)' : 'rgba(106,111,248,0.08)' }]}>
                                    <Ionicons name="layers-outline" size={12} color={colors.blue} />
                                    <Text style={[styles.metaText, { color: colors.blue }]}>GGUF</Text>
                                </View>
                                {model.likes > 0 && (
                                    <View style={[styles.metaBadge, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(228,216,77,0.12)' : 'rgba(228,216,77,0.08)' }]}>
                                        <Ionicons name="heart-outline" size={12} color={colors.gold} />
                                        <Text style={[styles.metaText, { color: colors.gold }]}>{formatDownloads(model.likes)}</Text>
                                    </View>
                                )}
                            </View>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                })}

                <View style={{ height: 40 }} />
            </ScrollView>
        </View>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',

    centerWrap: { alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: 60, gap: 10 },
    statusText: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium', marginTop: Spacing.sm },
    emptyText: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium', textAlign: 'center' },
    hintTitle: { fontSize: FontSize.lg, fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold', marginTop: Spacing.sm },
    hintText: {
        fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular',
        textAlign: 'center', lineHeight: 20, paddingHorizontal: Spacing.xl,
    },
    errorBox: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: Spacing.sm,
        padding: Spacing.md, borderRadius: BorderRadius.md, borderWidth: 1,
        marginBottom: Spacing.md,
    },
    errorText: { flex: 1, fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium' },

    resultAuthor: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', marginTop: 1 },
    resultMeta: {
        flexDirection: 'row', gap: Spacing.sm, marginTop: Spacing.md, flexWrap: 'wrap',

================ FILE: app/financial-health.js ================

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { View, Text, ScrollView, StyleSheet, TouchableOpacity, ActivityIndicator, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import Svg, { Circle, G, Text as SvgText } from 'react-native-svg';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { formatCurrency } from '../utils/currencies';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';
import { chatCompletion, isModelDownloaded } from '../services/localAI';
import { convertCurrency } from '../services/exchangeRates';

// Circular Progress Component
const ScoreGauge = ({ score, colors }) => {
    const size = 180;
    const strokeWidth = 14;
    const center = size / 2;
    const radius = center - strokeWidth;
    const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
    const progressOffset = circumference - (score / 100) * circumference;

    let scoreColor = '#ef4444'; // Red
    if (score >= 80) scoreColor = '#10b981'; // Green
    else if (score >= 50) scoreColor = '#f59e0b'; // Yellow

    return (
        <View style={styles.gaugeContainer}>
            <Svg width={size} height={size}>
                <G rotation="-90" origin={`${center}, ${center}`}>
                    <Circle
                        cx={center} cy={center} r={radius}
                        stroke={colors.border}
                        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
                        fill="transparent"
                    />
                    <Circle
                        cx={center} cy={center} r={radius}
                        stroke={scoreColor}
                        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
                        strokeDasharray={circumference}
                        strokeDashoffset={progressOffset}
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        fill="transparent"
                    />
                </G>
                <SvgText
                    x={center} y={center - 10}
                    textAnchor="middle" alignmentBaseline="central"
                    fill={colors.textPrimary}
                    fontSize="42" fontWeight="800" fontFamily="Poppins_800ExtraBold"
                >
                    {score}
                </SvgText>
                <SvgText
                    x={center} y={center + 24}
                    textAnchor="middle" alignmentBaseline="central"
                    fill={colors.textSecondary}
                    fontSize="14" fontWeight="600" fontFamily="Poppins_600SemiBold"
                >
                    / 100
                </SvgText>
            </Svg>
        </View>
    );
};

export default function FinancialHealth() {
    const router = useRouter();
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const { transactions, assets, savingsGoals, budgets, currency, exchangeRates } = useApp();

    const [modelReady, setModelReady] = useState(false);
    const [insight, setInsight] = useState('');
    const [generating, setGenerating] = useState(false);

    const [aiScore, setAiScore] = useState(null);
    const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState(new Date());

    useEffect(() => {
        isModelDownloaded().then(ready => setModelReady(ready));
    }, []);

    // Load cached insight for the selected month
    useEffect(() => {
        const loadCache = async () => {
            try {
                const year = selectedDate.getFullYear();
                const month = String(selectedDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                const key = `@financeflow_health_${year}_${month}`;
                const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(key);
                if (cached) {
                    const parsed = JSON.parse(cached);
                    setAiScore(parsed.score);
                    setInsight(parsed.insight);
                } else {
                    setAiScore(null);
                    setInsight('');
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.warn('Failed to load health cache', err);
            }
        };
        loadCache();
    }, [selectedDate]);

    const handlePrevMonth = () => {
        setSelectedDate(prev => new Date(prev.getFullYear(), prev.getMonth() - 1, 1));
    };

    const handleNextMonth = () => {
        if (isCurrentMonth) return;
        const now = new Date();
        const next = new Date(selectedDate.getFullYear(), selectedDate.getMonth() + 1, 1);
        if (next > now) return; // don't go to future
        setSelectedDate(next);
    };

    const isCurrentMonth = selectedDate.getFullYear() === new Date().getFullYear() && selectedDate.getMonth() === new Date().getMonth();

    // Calculate metrics
    const metrics = useMemo(() => {
        const year = selectedDate.getFullYear();
        const month = selectedDate.getMonth();

        // 1. Get live total asset value
        let liquidAssets = assets
            .filter(a => ['bank', 'cash', 'investment'].includes(a.type))
            .reduce((sum, a) => sum + convertCurrency(a.balance, a.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates), 0);

        const endOfSelectedMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0, 23, 59, 59, 999).getTime();

        // 2. Adjust live assets backwards by reverting future transactions
        transactions.forEach(t => {
            const txTime = new Date(t.date).getTime();
            if (txTime > endOfSelectedMonth) {
                const amt = convertCurrency(t.amount, t.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates);
                if (t.type === 'income') liquidAssets -= amt;
                if (t.type === 'spending') liquidAssets += amt;
                // 'transfer' does not change total net worth across liquid assets
            }
        });

        const savingsProgress = savingsGoals.reduce((sum, g) => sum + convertCurrency(g.currentAmount || 0, g.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates), 0);
        const currentSavings = liquidAssets + savingsProgress;

        // Cash flow for selected month
        let monthlyEarning = 0;
        let monthlySpending = 0;
        transactions.forEach(t => {
            const d = new Date(t.date);
            if (d.getFullYear() === year && d.getMonth() === month) {
                const amt = convertCurrency(t.amount, t.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates);
                if (t.type === 'income') monthlyEarning += amt;
                if (t.type === 'spending') monthlySpending += amt;
            }
        });

        const netSavings = monthlyEarning - monthlySpending;
        const savingRatio = monthlyEarning > 0 ? Math.max(0, (netSavings / monthlyEarning) * 100) : 0;

        // Monthly Budget Total
        const totalBudget = budgets ? budgets.reduce((sum, b) => sum + convertCurrency(b.amount || 0, b.currency || currency, currency, exchangeRates), 0) : 0;

        return {
            currentSavings,
            monthlyEarning,
            monthlySpending,
            savingRatio,
            totalBudget
        };
    }, [transactions, assets, savingsGoals, budgets, selectedDate, currency, exchangeRates]);

    const handleGenerateInsight = async () => {
        if (generating) return;
        if (!modelReady) {
            router.push('/settings');
            return;
        }

        // Prevent Fabric touch handler crashes on iOS by delaying state updates slightly
        setTimeout(async () => {
            setGenerating(true);
            setInsight('');
            setAiScore(null);

            const monthName = selectedDate.toLocaleString('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });

            const prompt = `You are a financial scoring system. You MUST output EXACTLY two lines.
Line 1: SCORE: [a single number between 0 and 100]
Line 2: ADVICE: [a single short sentence of advice]
Do NOT output any thinking, greetings, markdown, or bullet points.

Data for ${monthName}:
- Assets: ${formatCurrency(metrics.currentSavings, currency)}
- Earned: ${formatCurrency(metrics.monthlyEarning, currency)}
- Spent: ${formatCurrency(metrics.monthlySpending, currency)}
- Budget: ${formatCurrency(metrics.totalBudget, currency)}
- Savings Ratio: ${metrics.savingRatio.toFixed(1)}%`;

            let fullText = '';
            try {
                await chatCompletion([{ role: 'user', content: prompt }], (token) => {
                    fullText += token;
                });

                let finalScore = null;
                let finalAdvice = '';

                // Attempt 1: Strict JSON (just in case it still outputs JSON)
                const jsonMatch = fullText.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
                if (jsonMatch) {
                    try {
                        const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
                        if (typeof parsed.score === 'number') finalScore = parsed.score;
                        if (parsed.advice) finalAdvice = parsed.advice;
                    } catch (e) { }
                }

                // Attempt 2: Strict Labels "SCORE: XX"
                if (finalScore === null) {
                    const scoreMatch = fullText.match(/SCORE\s*[:=|-]?\s*(\d{1,3})/i);
                    if (scoreMatch) finalScore = parseInt(scoreMatch[1], 10);
                }

                // Attempt 3: Look for the word score followed by a number
                if (finalScore === null) {
                    const nearScore = fullText.match(/score.*?(\d{1,3})/i);
                    if (nearScore) finalScore = parseInt(nearScore[1], 10);
                }

                // Extract Advice
                if (!finalAdvice) {
                    const adviceMatch = fullText.match(/ADVICE\s*[:=|-]?\s*([\s\S]*)/i);
                    if (adviceMatch && adviceMatch[1].trim().length > 5) {
                        finalAdvice = adviceMatch[1].trim();
                    } else {
                        // Clean up raw text
                        let cleaned = fullText
                            .replace(/\*+/g, '') // remove markdown bold/italics
                            .replace(/SCORE\s*[:=|-]?\s*\d{1,3}/i, '') // remove score line
                            .replace(/#+/g, '') // remove headings
                            .replace(/- /g, '') // remove bullet points
                            .replace(/^\d+\.\s.*$/gm, '') // remove numbered lists like "1. Evaluate metrics"
                            .replace(/evaluate.*metrics/i, '')
                            .trim();
                        finalAdvice = cleaned || "Keep building your financial foundation.";
                    }
                }

                setAiScore(finalScore !== null ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, finalScore)) : 50); // fallback to 50 if totally failed
                setInsight(finalAdvice);

                // Cache the result
                try {
                    const cacheYear = selectedDate.getFullYear();
                    const cacheMonth = String(selectedDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                    await AsyncStorage.setItem(`@financeflow_health_${cacheYear}_${cacheMonth}`, JSON.stringify({
                        score: finalScore !== null ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, finalScore)) : 50,
                        insight: finalAdvice
                    }));
                } catch (e) { }

            } catch (error) {
                setInsight("Unable to generate insight right now. Please ensure the AI model is ready in Settings.");
            } finally {
                setGenerating(false);
            }
        }, 50);
    };

    return (
        <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: Platform.OS === 'android' ? insets.top : 0 }]}>
            <View style={[styles.handle, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />

            {/* Header */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => { if (router.canDismiss?.()) { router.dismiss(); } else { router.back(); } }}
                    style={[
                        styles.backBtn,
                        {
                            backgroundColor: colors.card,
                            borderColor: colors.border,
                            borderWidth: 1,
                        },
                    ]}
                >
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Financial Health</Text>
                <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
            </View>

            <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>

                {/* Month Selector */}
                <View style={[styles.monthSelector, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                    <TouchableOpacity onPress={handlePrevMonth} style={styles.monthBtn}>
                        <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <Text style={[styles.monthText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                        {selectedDate.toLocaleString('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })}
                    </Text>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={handleNextMonth}
                        style={styles.monthBtn}
                    >
                        <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={20} color={isCurrentMonth ? colors.border : colors.primary} />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>

                {/* Gauge Section */}
                <View style={[styles.card, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                    <ScoreGauge score={aiScore ?? 0} colors={colors} />
                    <Text style={[styles.gaugeDesc, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                        {aiScore === null
                            ? "Tap Analyze to let AI determine your score!"
                            : aiScore >= 80 ? "Excellent! Your finances are in great shape."
                                : aiScore >= 50 ? "Good work! You have a solid foundation."
                                    : "Needs Attention. Let's work on improving your habits."}
                    </Text>
                </View>

                {/* Metrics */}
                <View style={[styles.metricsGrid]}>
                    <View style={[styles.metricBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                        <View style={[styles.iconWrap, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(5, 150, 105, 0.1)' }]}>
                            <Ionicons name="wallet" size={20} color="#059669" />
                        </View>
                        <Text style={[styles.metricLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Total Assets</Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.metricValue, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{formatCurrency(metrics.currentSavings, currency)}</Text>
                    </View>

                    <View style={[styles.metricBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                        <View style={[styles.iconWrap, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.1)' }]}>
                            <Ionicons name="pie-chart" size={20} color="#3b82f6" />
                        </View>
                        <Text style={[styles.metricLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Savings Ratio</Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.metricValue, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{metrics.savingRatio.toFixed(1)}%</Text>
                    </View>
                </View>

                <View style={[styles.card, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                    <View style={styles.rowBetween}>
                        <View style={styles.flowRow}>
                            <View style={[styles.flowDot, { backgroundColor: '#10b981' }]} />
                            <Text style={[styles.flowLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Earned</Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.flowValue, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{formatCurrency(metrics.monthlyEarning, currency)}</Text>
                        </View>
                        <View style={styles.flowRow}>
                            <View style={[styles.flowDot, { backgroundColor: '#ef4444' }]} />
                            <Text style={[styles.flowLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Spent</Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.flowValue, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{formatCurrency(metrics.monthlySpending, currency)}</Text>
                        </View>
                        <View style={styles.flowRow}>
                            <View style={[styles.flowDot, { backgroundColor: '#f59e0b' }]} />
                            <Text style={[styles.flowLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Budget</Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.flowValue, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{formatCurrency(metrics.totalBudget, currency)}</Text>
                        </View>
                    </View>
                </View>

                {/* AI Insights Section */}
                <View style={[styles.aiSection, { backgroundColor: isDark ? 'rgba(83,96,132,0.1)' : 'rgba(255,102,0,0.05)', borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                    <View style={styles.aiHeader}>
                        <Ionicons name="sparkles" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                        <Text style={[styles.aiTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>AI Financial Advisor</Text>
                    </View>

                    {insight ? (
                        <View style={styles.insightBox}>
                            <Text style={[styles.insightText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{insight}</Text>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                style={{ marginTop: 12, alignSelf: 'flex-start', flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}
                                onPress={handleGenerateInsight}
                                disabled={generating}
                            >
                                {generating ? <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colors.primary} /> : <Ionicons name="refresh" size={16} color={colors.primary} />}
                                <Text style={{ color: colors.primary, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium', fontSize: 13 }}>
                                    {generating ? 'Regenerating...' : 'Regenerate Insight'}
                                </Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>
                    ) : (
                        <View style={styles.emptyAiBox}>
                            <Text style={[styles.emptyAiText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                                Get personalized, AI-driven advice on how to improve your financial health score.
                            </Text>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                style={[styles.aiBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.primary, opacity: generating ? 0.7 : 1 }]}
                                onPress={handleGenerateInsight}
                            >
                                {generating ? (
                                    <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#fff" />
                                ) : (
                                    <Ionicons name={modelReady ? "flash" : "download"} size={18} color="#fff" />
                                )}
                                <Text style={styles.aiBtnText}>
                                    {generating ? 'Analyzing...' : modelReady ? 'Analyze My Health' : 'Download AI Model'}
                                </Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>
                    )}
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: 12, lineHeight: 16 }}>
                        Disclaimer: AI-generated scores and insights are for informational purposes only and do not constitute financial, investment, or tax advice.
                    </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={{ height: 40 }} />
            </ScrollView>
        </View>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1 },
    handle: {
        width: 36,
        height: 4,
        borderRadius: 2,
        backgroundColor: "rgba(83,96,132,0.2)",
        alignSelf: "center",
        marginTop: Spacing.md,
    },
    header: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg, paddingVertical: Spacing.md,
    },
    backBtn: {
        width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 14,
        alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
    },
    title: { fontSize: FontSize.xl, fontFamily: 'Poppins_800ExtraBold' },
    scrollContent: { padding: Spacing.lg },

    monthSelector: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
        padding: Spacing.sm, borderRadius: BorderRadius.full, borderWidth: 1,
        marginBottom: Spacing.lg,
    },
    monthBtn: { width: 40, height: 40, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
    monthText: { fontSize: FontSize.md, fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold' },

    card: {
        borderRadius: 24, borderWidth: 1,
        padding: Spacing.xl,
        marginBottom: Spacing.md,
        alignItems: 'center',
    },
    gaugeContainer: { alignItems: 'center', marginVertical: Spacing.sm },
    gaugeDesc: {
        fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium',
        textAlign: 'center', marginTop: Spacing.md, lineHeight: 20
    },

    metricsGrid: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: Spacing.md, marginBottom: Spacing.md },
    metricBox: {
        flex: 1, borderRadius: 24, borderWidth: 1,
        padding: Spacing.lg, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
    },
    iconWrap: { width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 22, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: Spacing.sm },
    metricLabel: { fontSize: FontSize.xs, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium', marginBottom: 2 },
    metricValue: { fontSize: FontSize.md, fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold' },

    rowBetween: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', width: '100%', paddingHorizontal: Spacing.sm },
    flowRow: { alignItems: 'center' },
    flowDot: { width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: 4, marginBottom: 4 },
    flowLabel: { fontSize: FontSize.xs, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium', marginBottom: 2 },
    flowValue: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold' },

    aiSection: {
        borderRadius: 24, borderWidth: 1, padding: Spacing.xl, marginTop: Spacing.sm,
    },
    aiHeader: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: Spacing.sm, marginBottom: Spacing.md },
    aiTitle: { fontSize: FontSize.md, fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold' },
    emptyAiBox: { alignItems: 'flex-start' },
    emptyAiText: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', lineHeight: 22, marginBottom: Spacing.md },
    aiBtn: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: Spacing.sm,
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg, paddingVertical: 12, borderRadius: BorderRadius.full,
    },
    aiBtnText: { color: '#fff', fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold', fontSize: FontSize.sm },
    insightBox: { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.03)', padding: Spacing.md, borderRadius: BorderRadius.md },
    insightText: { fontSize: FontSize.sm, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium', lineHeight: 24 }
});


================ FILE: app/log.js ================

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
    View,
    Text,
    StyleSheet,
    TouchableOpacity,
    FlatList,
    TextInput,
    Alert,
    ActivityIndicator,
    Platform,
    ScrollView,
    useWindowDimensions,
} from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';
import { getLogs, clearLogs, exportLogs, subscribeToLogs } from '../services/appLogger';

const CATEGORY_FILTERS = [
    { id: 'all', label: 'All', icon: 'layers-outline' },
    { id: 'transaction', label: 'Transactions', icon: 'receipt-outline' },
    { id: 'asset', label: 'Accounts', icon: 'wallet-outline' },
    { id: 'currency', label: 'Currency', icon: 'cash-outline' },
    { id: 'error', label: 'Errors', icon: 'alert-circle-outline' },
    { id: 'system', label: 'System', icon: 'cog-outline' },
];

export default function LogScreen() {
    const router = useRouter();
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();

    const [logs, setLogs] = useState([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
    const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState('all');
    const [expandedLogId, setExpandedLogId] = useState(null);
    const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState(false);

    const loadLogsData = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const data = await getLogs();
            setLogs(data);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Failed to load logs:', e);
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        loadLogsData();
        const unsubscribe = subscribeToLogs((newLogs) => {
            setLogs(newLogs);
        });
        return unsubscribe;
    }, [loadLogsData]);

    const handleClearLogs = () => {
        if (logs.length === 0) return;
        Alert.alert(
            'Clear Activity Logs',
            'Are you sure you want to delete all local activity and system logs? This action cannot be undone.',
            [
                { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
                {
                    text: 'Clear All',
                    style: 'destructive',
                    onPress: async () => {
                        await clearLogs();
                        setLogs([]);
                    },
                },
            ]
        );
    };

    const handleExportLogs = async () => {
        if (logs.length === 0) {
            Alert.alert('No Logs', 'There are no logs to export yet.');
            return;
        }
        setIsExporting(true);
        try {
            const result = await exportLogs();
            if (!result.success && result.reason !== 'sharing_unavailable') {
                Alert.alert('Export Failed', result.reason || 'Could not export logs.');
            }
        } catch (e) {
            Alert.alert('Export Error', e.message);
        } finally {
            setIsExporting(false);
        }
    };

    const filteredLogs = useMemo(() => {
        return logs.filter((log) => {
            // Category filter
            if (selectedCategory !== 'all') {
                if (selectedCategory === 'error' && log.level !== 'error' && log.category !== 'error') {
                    return false;
                } else if (selectedCategory !== 'error' && log.category !== selectedCategory) {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            // Search query filter
            if (search.trim()) {
                const query = search.toLowerCase();
                const titleMatch = (log.title || '').toLowerCase().includes(query);
                const detailsMatch = (log.details || '').toLowerCase().includes(query);
                const categoryMatch = (log.category || '').toLowerCase().includes(query);
                return titleMatch || detailsMatch || categoryMatch;
            }

            return true;
        });
    }, [logs, selectedCategory, search]);

    const getLevelColors = (level, category) => {
        if (level === 'error' || category === 'error') {
            return {
                bg: isDark ? `${colors.spending}22` : `${colors.spending}14`,
                border: `${colors.spending}40`,
                color: colors.spending,
                icon: 'alert-circle',
            };
        }
        if (level === 'warn') {
            return {
                bg: isDark ? `${colors.gold}22` : `${colors.gold}18`,
                border: `${colors.gold}44`,
                color: colors.gold,
                icon: 'warning',
            };
        }
        if (level === 'success') {
            return {
                bg: isDark ? `${colors.income}22` : `${colors.income}16`,
                border: `${colors.income}40`,
                color: colors.income,
                icon: 'checkmark-circle',
            };
        }
        return {
            bg: isDark ? `${colors.blue}22` : `${colors.blue}14`,
            border: `${colors.blue}40`,
            color: colors.blue,
            icon: 'information-circle',
        };
    };

    const getCategoryIcon = (category) => {
        switch (category) {
            case 'transaction':
                return 'receipt-outline';
            case 'asset':
                return 'wallet-outline';
            case 'currency':
                return 'cash-outline';
            case 'settings':
                return 'cog-outline';
            case 'backup':
                return 'cloud-outline';
            case 'ai':
                return 'sparkles-outline';
            case 'error':
                return 'bug-outline';
            default:
                return 'document-text-outline';
        }
    };

    const formatTimestamp = (iso) => {
        if (!iso) return '';
        try {
            const d = new Date(iso);
            return `${d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })} at ${d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' })}`;
        } catch (e) {
            return iso;
        }
    };

    const SectionHeader = ({ icon, color, title }) => (
        <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
            <View style={[styles.sectionIconWrap, { backgroundColor: `${color}15` }]}>
                <Ionicons name={icon} size={18} color={color} />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{title}</Text>
        </View>
    );

    const renderLogItem = ({ item }) => {
        const badge = getLevelColors(item.level, item.category);
        const catIcon = getCategoryIcon(item.category);
        const isExpanded = expandedLogId === item.id;

        return (
            <View style={[styles.logCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
                <View style={styles.logMain}>
                    <View style={[styles.logIcon, { backgroundColor: badge.bg }]}>
                        <Ionicons name={catIcon} size={20} color={badge.color} />
                    </View>
                    <View style={styles.logInfo}>
                        <View style={styles.logTitleRow}>
                            <Text style={[styles.logName, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                {item.title}
                            </Text>
                            <View style={[styles.levelBadge, { backgroundColor: badge.bg, borderColor: badge.border }]}>
                                <Text style={[styles.levelBadgeText, { color: badge.color }]}>
                                    {(item.level || 'info').toUpperCase()}
                                </Text>
                            </View>
                        </View>
                        <Text style={[styles.logMeta, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                            {item.category?.toUpperCase()} · {formatTimestamp(item.timestamp)}
                        </Text>
                    </View>
                </View>

                {/* Details text */}
                {!!item.details && (
                    <Text style={[styles.logDetails, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                        {item.details}
                    </Text>
                )}

                {/* Stack trace toggle for errors */}
                {!!item.stack && (
                    <View style={styles.stackWrap}>
                        <TouchableOpacity
                            style={[styles.stackToggleBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: colors.border }]}
                            onPress={() => setExpandedLogId(isExpanded ? null : item.id)}
                            activeOpacity={0.7}
                        >
                            <Ionicons
                                name={isExpanded ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'}
                                size={14}
                                color={colors.primary}
                            />
                            <Text style={[styles.stackToggleText, { color: colors.primary }]}>
                                {isExpanded ? 'Hide Stack Trace' : 'View Stack Trace'}
                            </Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>

                        {isExpanded && (
                            <View style={[styles.stackBox, { backgroundColor: isDark ? '#16171F' : '#F1F3F7', borderColor: colors.border }]}>
                                <Text style={[styles.stackText, { color: colors.textPrimary }]} selectable>
                                    {item.stack}
                                </Text>
                            </View>
                        )}
                    </View>
                )}
            </View>
        );
    };

    return (
        <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
            {/* Background SVG Gradient */}
            <Svg key={colors.background} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject} width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} pointerEvents="none">
                <Defs>
                    <SvgGrad id="bgGradLog" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                        <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                    </SvgGrad>
                </Defs>
                <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} fill="url(#bgGradLog)" />
            </Svg>

            {/* Top Navigation Bar */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => router.back()}
                    style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                    activeOpacity={0.75}
                    accessibilityLabel="Back to settings"
                >
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={colors.primary} />
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Activity Log</Text>

                <View style={styles.headerActions}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={handleExportLogs}
                        style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                        activeOpacity={0.75}
                        disabled={isExporting}
                        accessibilityLabel="Export logs"
                    >
                        {isExporting ? (
                            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={colors.primary} />
                        ) : (
                            <Ionicons name="share-outline" size={18} color={colors.primary} />
                        )}
                    </TouchableOpacity>

                    <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={handleClearLogs}
                        style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                        activeOpacity={0.75}
                        accessibilityLabel="Clear logs"
                    >
                        <Ionicons
                            name="trash-outline"
                            size={18}
                            color={logs.length > 0 ? (isDark ? '#FF8594' : '#dc2626') : colors.textMuted}
                        />
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
            </View>

            {/* Privacy Callout Section */}
            <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg }}>
                <View style={[styles.section, { backgroundColor: colors.card, marginBottom: Spacing.sm }]}>
                    <SectionHeader icon="shield-checkmark" color={colors.emerald} title="100% On-Device & Private" />
                    <Text style={[styles.helperText, { color: colors.textSecondary, marginBottom: 0 }]}>
                        Activity history and diagnostic traces are saved strictly in local sandboxed storage and never transmitted to external servers.
                    </Text>
                </View>
            </View>

            {/* Search Input Box */}
            <View style={styles.searchRow}>
                <View style={[styles.searchBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 }]}>
                    <Ionicons name="search" size={16} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                    <TextInput
                        style={[styles.searchInput, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}
                        placeholder="Search logs, actions, details…"
                        placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                        value={search}
                        onChangeText={setSearch}
                        autoCorrect={false}
                    />
                    {search ? (
                        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearch('')}>
                            <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={16} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    ) : null}
                </View>
            </View>

            {/* Filter Chips Row */}
            <View style={styles.filterRow}>
                <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.filterChipRow}>
                    {CATEGORY_FILTERS.map((opt) => {
                        const isSelected = selectedCategory === opt.id;
                        return (
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                key={opt.id}
                                style={[
                                    styles.filterChip,
                                    { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border, borderWidth: 1 },
                                    isSelected && { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight, borderColor: colors.primary },
                                ]}
                                onPress={() => setSelectedCategory(opt.id)}
                                activeOpacity={0.8}
                            >
                                <Ionicons
                                    name={opt.icon}
                                    size={14}
                                    color={isSelected ? colors.primary : colors.textSecondary}
                                />
                                <Text
                                    style={[
                                        styles.filterChipText,
                                        { color: isSelected ? colors.primary : colors.textSecondary },
                                        isSelected && { fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold' },
                                    ]}
                                >
                                    {opt.label}
                                </Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        );
                    })}
                </ScrollView>
            </View>

            {/* Logs List / Empty State */}
            {loading ? (
                <View style={styles.centerContainer}>
                    <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
                </View>
            ) : filteredLogs.length === 0 ? (
                <View style={styles.emptyState}>
                    <View style={[styles.emptyIconWrap, { backgroundColor: colors.primaryLight }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="receipt-outline" size={38} color={colors.primary} />
                    </View>
                    <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>No Logs Found</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.emptyDesc, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                        {search.trim() || selectedCategory !== 'all'
                            ? 'No activity logs match your current search or category filter.'
                            : 'App operations, transactions, accounts, and system diagnostics will automatically appear here.'}
                    </Text>
                </View>
            ) : (
                <FlatList
                    data={filteredLogs}
                    keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
                    renderItem={renderLogItem}
                    contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
                    showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
                    initialNumToRender={12}
                    maxToRenderPerBatch={10}
                    windowSize={5}
                />
            )}
        </View>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

================ FILE: app/ocr-info.js ================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
    View,
    Text,
    ScrollView,
    TouchableOpacity,
    StyleSheet,
    Platform,
    useWindowDimensions,
} from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';

export default function OCRInfoScreen() {
    const router = useRouter();
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();

    const [expandedCards, setExpandedCards] = useState({});

    const toggleCard = (index) => {
        setExpandedCards(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            [index]: !prev[index],
        }));
    };

    const QA_LIST = [
        {
            id: 0,
            question: "What is On-Device OCR?",
            category: "Basics",
            categoryColor: "#F59E0B",
            icon: "eye-outline",
            shortAnswer: "Optical Character Recognition running directly on your phone's native hardware without internet.",
            fullAnswer: [
                "OCR stands for Optical Character Recognition. It is a computer vision technology that detects and extracts printed alphanumeric characters from photos.",
                "In FinanceFlow, Instant Scan uses native on-device frameworks (Apple Vision framework on iOS and Google ML Kit on Android). Your receipt is never uploaded to any remote server or external cloud.",
            ],
            points: [
                { title: "Lightning Fast", desc: "Extracts text in under 1 second without waiting for cloud queues.", icon: "flash" },
                { title: "100% Private", desc: "All receipt data and prices stay strictly on your phone.", icon: "lock-closed" },
                { title: "Works Offline", desc: "Scan receipts even in airplane mode or areas without cellular signal.", icon: "cloud-offline" },
            ],
        },
        {
            id: 1,
            question: "How does the feature work in FinanceFlow?",
            category: "Architecture",
            categoryColor: "#059669",
            icon: "cog-outline",
            shortAnswer: "3-step pipeline: Text Recognition → Geometric Line Reconstruction → Smart Regex Parsing.",
            fullAnswer: [
                "FinanceFlow's OCR pipeline transforms a raw photograph into structured transaction data through three deterministic phases:",
            ],
            steps: [
                {
                    num: "1",
                    title: "Hardware Vision Extraction",
                    desc: "The camera captures text bounding boxes with pixel coordinates and confidence scores directly from iOS/Android hardware.",
                },
                {
                    num: "2",
                    title: "Adaptive Line Reconstruction",
                    desc: "Our geometric engine computes horizontal alignment, detects tilt angles, and merges multi-column item names, quantities, and prices.",
                },
                {
                    num: "3",
                    title: "Deterministic Financial Parser",
                    desc: "Recognizes store brands, transaction dates, taxable amounts, taxes (SST, GST, VAT), service fees, and grand totals.",
                },
            ],
        },
        {
            id: 2,
            question: "Instant Scan (OCR) vs. AI Vision Scan: What is the difference?",
            category: "Comparison",
            categoryColor: "#6366F1",
            icon: "git-compare-outline",
            shortAnswer: "Instant Scan prioritizes raw speed and offline privacy; AI Vision excels at complex or degraded receipts.",
            comparison: [
                {
                    feature: "Scan Speed",
                    ocr: "⚡ < 1 Second (Instant)",
                    ai: "⏳ 3 – 8 Seconds",
                },
                {
                    feature: "Internet Required",
                    ocr: "❌ No (100% Offline)",
                    ai: "Depends on model mode",
                },
                {
                    feature: "Receipt Type",
                    ocr: "Standard printed receipts",
                    ai: "Handwritten & complex layouts",
                },
                {
                    feature: "Battery & CPU",
                    ocr: "Near-zero consumption",
                    ai: "Higher compute usage",
                },
                {
                    feature: "Handling Faded Ink",
                    ocr: "Requires legible contrast",
                    ai: "High contextual tolerance",
                },
            ],

            ],

                {

    return (

                        const isExpanded = !!expandedCards[item.id];
                        return (
                            <View
                                key={item.id}
                                style={[
                                    styles.qaCard,
                                    {
                                        backgroundColor: colors.card,
                                        borderColor: isExpanded ? colors.primaryLight || colors.border : colors.border,

                                    style={styles.qaCardHeader}

                                        <View style={[styles.qaIconCircle, { backgroundColor: `${item.categoryColor}18` }]}>
                                            <Ionicons name={item.icon} size={20} color={item.categoryColor} />
                                        </View>
