});

================ FILE: app/vehicle/[id].js ================

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
    View, Text, StyleSheet, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity,
    Alert, Platform, useWindowDimensions
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useApp } from '../../context/AppContext';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { formatCurrency } from '../../utils/currencies';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../../constants/theme';

function formatTime(seconds) {
    if (!seconds) return '—';
    const h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
    const m = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
    return h > 0 ? `${h}h ${m}m` : `${m}m`;
}

export default function VehicleDashboard() {
    const { id } = useLocalSearchParams();
    const router = useRouter();
    const { vehicles, transactions, currency, deleteVehicle, deleteTransaction } = useApp();
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

    const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'refuel' | 'maintenance'
    const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState(null);

    const vehicle = useMemo(() => vehicles.find(v => v.id === id), [vehicles, id]);

    const vehicleTransactions = useMemo(() => {
        return transactions
            .filter(t => t.vehicleId === id)
            .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));
    }, [transactions, id]);

    const filteredTransactions = useMemo(() => {
        if (filter === 'all') return vehicleTransactions;
        return vehicleTransactions.filter(t => t.vehicleLogType === filter);
    }, [vehicleTransactions, filter]);

    // Summary stats
    const stats = useMemo(() => {
        const refuels = [...vehicleTransactions]
            .filter(t => t.vehicleLogType === 'refuel')
            .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date));

        let totalVolume = 0;
        let totalRefuelCost = 0;
        let totalMaintenanceCost = 0;
        let latestOdo = vehicle?.initialOdometer || 0;

        refuels.forEach(r => {
            totalVolume += (r.vehicleVolume || 0);
            totalRefuelCost += Math.abs(r.amount || 0);
            if ((r.vehicleOdometer || 0) > latestOdo) latestOdo = r.vehicleOdometer;
        });

        vehicleTransactions.filter(t => t.vehicleLogType === 'maintenance').forEach(m => {
            totalMaintenanceCost += Math.abs(m.amount || 0);
            if ((m.vehicleOdometer || 0) > latestOdo) latestOdo = m.vehicleOdometer;
        });

        const firstOdo = vehicle?.initialOdometer || refuels[0]?.vehicleOdometer || 0;
        const lastOdo = refuels[refuels.length - 1]?.vehicleOdometer || 0;
        const totalDistance = Math.max(0, lastOdo - firstOdo);
        const avgEfficiency = totalVolume > 0 && totalDistance > 0 ? totalDistance / totalVolume : 0;
        const totalCost = totalRefuelCost + totalMaintenanceCost;
        const costPerKm = totalDistance > 0 ? totalCost / totalDistance : 0;

        return { totalCost, totalVolume, totalDistance, latestOdo, avgEfficiency, costPerKm, refuelCount: refuels.length };
    }, [vehicleTransactions, vehicle]);

    // Build enriched transaction list with computed per-tx metrics
    const enrichedTransactions = useMemo(() => {
        const refuels = [...vehicleTransactions]
            .filter(t => t.vehicleLogType === 'refuel')
            .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date));

        const refuelWithPrev = refuels.map((r, idx) => {
            const prevOdo = idx === 0
                ? (vehicle?.initialOdometer || null)
                : (refuels[idx - 1]?.vehicleOdometer || null);
            return { ...r, _prevOdo: prevOdo };
        });
        const refuelMap = Object.fromEntries(refuelWithPrev.map(r => [r.id, r]));

        return filteredTransactions.map(tx => ({
            ...tx,
            _prevOdo: refuelMap[tx.id]?._prevOdo ?? null
        }));
    }, [filteredTransactions, vehicleTransactions, vehicle]);

    if (!vehicle) {
        return (
            <View style={[s.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: Platform.OS === 'android' ? insets.top : 0 }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: colors.textPrimary, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium', fontSize: FontSize.md }}>Vehicle not found.</Text>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => router.back()} style={{ marginTop: 20 }}>
                    <Text style={{ color: colors.primary, fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold', fontSize: FontSize.sm }}>Go Back</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
        );
    }

    const handleDeleteVehicle = () => {
        Alert.alert('Delete Vehicle', 'Are you sure? Its transaction history will be kept but will lose the vehicle link.', [
            { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
            { text: 'Delete', style: 'destructive', onPress: async () => { await deleteVehicle(id); router.back(); } }
        ]);
    };

    const confirmDeleteTx = (tx) => {
        Alert.alert('Delete Record', 'Delete this record?', [
            { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
            { text: 'Delete', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => deleteTransaction(tx.id) }
        ]);
    };

    const isEV = vehicle.type === 'ev';
    const volUnit = isEV ? 'kWh' : 'L';
    const effUnit = isEV ? 'km/kWh' : 'km/L';
    const purple = colors.primary;
    const textPrimary = colors.textPrimary;
    const textSec = colors.textSecondary;
    const border = colors.border;
    const cardBg = colors.card;

    return (
        <View style={[s.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: Platform.OS === 'android' ? insets.top : 0 }]}>
            {/* Gradient background */}
            <Svg key={colors.background} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject} width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} pointerEvents="none">
                <Defs>
                    <SvgGrad id="vBg" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                        <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                    </SvgGrad>
                </Defs>
                <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} fill="url(#vBg)" />
            </Svg>

            <View style={[s.handle, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />

            {/* Header */}
            <View style={s.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => { if (router.canDismiss?.()) { router.dismiss(); } else { router.back(); } }}
                    style={[s.headerIconBtn, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: border, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                >
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={purple} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <View style={s.headerCenter}>
                    <View style={[s.headerAvatar, { backgroundColor: isEV ? '#10B981' : purple }]}>
                        <Ionicons name={isEV ? 'flash' : 'car-sport'} size={16} color="#fff" />
                    </View>
                    <View>
                        <Text style={[s.headerTitle, { color: textPrimary }]}>{vehicle.name}</Text>
                        <Text style={[s.headerSub, { color: textSec }]}>{isEV ? 'Electric Vehicle' : 'Gasoline / Diesel'}</Text>
                    </View>
                </View>
                <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={handleDeleteVehicle}
                    style={[s.headerIconBtn, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.1)', borderColor: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.2)', borderWidth: 1 }]}
                >
                    <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={18} color="#ef4444" />
                </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                {/* Stats grid – transparent, no border */}
                <View style={s.kpiGrid}>
                    <View style={[s.kpiCard, { backgroundColor: 'transparent', borderWidth: 0 }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="speedometer-outline" size={18} color={purple} />
                        <Text style={[s.kpiVal, { color: textPrimary }]}>
                            {stats.latestOdo > 0 ? stats.latestOdo.toLocaleString() : '—'}
                        </Text>
                        <Text style={[s.kpiLbl, { color: textSec }]}>Odometer (km)</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[s.kpiCard, { backgroundColor: 'transparent', borderWidth: 0 }]}>
                        <Ionicons name={isEV ? 'flash-outline' : 'water-outline'} size={18} color={isEV ? '#10B981' : '#ef4444'} />
                        <Text style={[s.kpiVal, { color: stats.avgEfficiency > 0 ? '#10B981' : textPrimary }]}>
                            {stats.avgEfficiency > 0 ? stats.avgEfficiency.toFixed(2) : '—'}
                        </Text>
                        <Text style={[s.kpiLbl, { color: textSec }]}>Avg {effUnit}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[s.kpiCard, { backgroundColor: 'transparent', borderWidth: 0 }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="trending-up-outline" size={18} color="#f59e0b" />
                        <Text style={[s.kpiVal, { color: textPrimary }]}>
                            {stats.costPerKm > 0 ? formatCurrency(stats.costPerKm, currency) : '—'}
                        </Text>
                        <Text style={[s.kpiLbl, { color: textSec }]}>Cost / km</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[s.kpiCard, { backgroundColor: 'transparent', borderWidth: 0 }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="wallet-outline" size={18} color="#3b82f6" />
                        <Text style={[s.kpiVal, { color: '#ef4444' }]}>
                            {formatCurrency(stats.totalCost, currency)}
                        </Text>
                        <Text style={[s.kpiLbl, { color: textSec }]}>Total Spent</Text>
                    </View>
                </View>



                {/* Filter chips */}
                <View style={s.sortRow}>
                    <Text style={[s.sortLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>Filter:</Text>
                    <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={s.sortChipRow}>
                        {[{ key: 'all', label: 'All Records' }, { key: 'refuel', label: 'Refueling' }, { key: 'maintenance', label: 'Maintenance' }].map(f => (
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                key={f.key}
                                style={[
                                    s.sortChip,
                                    { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.border },
                                    filter === f.key && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.12)', borderColor: colors.primary }
                                ]}
                                onPress={() => setFilter(f.key)}
                            >
                                <Text style={[
                                    s.sortChipText,
                                    { color: colors.textSecondary },
                                    filter === f.key && { color: colors.primary }
                                ]}>{f.label}</Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        ))}
                    </ScrollView>
                </View>

                {/* Transaction list */}
                <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg }}>
                    {enrichedTransactions.length === 0 ? (
                        <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: 48 }}>
                            <Ionicons name="document-text-outline" size={52} color={border} />
                            <Text style={{ color: textSec, marginTop: Spacing.md, fontFamily: 'Poppins_500Medium', fontSize: FontSize.md }}>
                                No records found
                            </Text>
                            <Text style={{ color: textSec, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular', fontSize: FontSize.sm, marginTop: 4 }}>
                                Add a transaction in the Car category.
                            </Text>
                        </View>
                    ) : (
                        enrichedTransactions.map((tx) => {
                            const isRefuel = tx.vehicleLogType === 'refuel';
                            const amount = Math.abs(tx.amount || 0);
                            const expanded = expandedId === tx.id;

                            // Per-refuel metrics
                            const distanceDriven = (isRefuel && tx._prevOdo != null && tx.vehicleOdometer)
                                ? Math.max(0, tx.vehicleOdometer - tx._prevOdo)
                                : null;
                            const efficiency = (isRefuel && distanceDriven != null && tx.vehicleVolume > 0)
                                ? distanceDriven / tx.vehicleVolume
                                : null;
                            const pricePerUnit = (isRefuel && tx.vehicleVolume > 0)
                                ? amount / tx.vehicleVolume
                                : null;
                            const costPerKm = (isRefuel && distanceDriven != null && distanceDriven > 0)
                                ? amount / distanceDriven
                                : null;

                            const formattedDate = new Date(tx.date).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { year: 'numeric', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });

                            return (
                                <TouchableOpacity
                                    key={tx.id}
                                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                                    style={[s.txCard, { backgroundColor: cardBg }]}
                                    onPress={() => setExpandedId(expanded ? null : tx.id)}
                                >
                                    {/* Card top row */}
                                    <View style={s.txTop}>
                                        <View style={[s.txIconWrap, { backgroundColor: isRefuel ? 'rgba(239,68,68,0.1)' : 'rgba(245,158,11,0.1)' }]}>
                                            <Ionicons
                                                name={isRefuel ? (isEV ? 'flash-outline' : 'water-outline') : 'build-outline'}
                                                size={20}
                                                color={isRefuel ? (isEV ? '#10B981' : '#ef4444') : '#f59e0b'}
                                            />
                                        </View>
                                        <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: Spacing.sm }}>
                                            <Text style={[s.txType, { color: textPrimary }]}>
                                                {isRefuel ? (isEV ? 'Charging' : 'Refuel') : 'Maintenance'}
                                            </Text>
                                            <Text style={[s.txDate, { color: textSec }]}>{formattedDate}</Text>
                                        </View>
                                        <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end', gap: 4 }}>
                                            <Text style={s.txAmount}>−{formatCurrency(amount, currency)}</Text>
                                            <Ionicons
                                                name={expanded ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'}
                                                size={14}
                                                color={textSec}
                                            />
                                        </View>
                                    </View>

                                    {/* Collapsed: compact summary */}
                                    {!expanded && (
                                        <View style={s.compactInfoBlock}>
                                            {isRefuel ? (
                                                <>
                                                    <View style={s.compactRow}>
                                                        <View style={s.compactPill}>
                                                            <Ionicons name={isEV ? 'flash-outline' : 'water-outline'} size={12} color={isEV ? '#10B981' : '#ef4444'} />
                                                            <Text style={[s.compactTxt, { color: textSec }]}>
                                                                {tx.vehicleVolume ? `${tx.vehicleVolume} ${volUnit}` : '—'}
                                                            </Text>
                                                            <Text style={[s.compactSep, { color: textSec }]}>→</Text>
                                                            <Text style={[s.compactTxt, { color: textSec }]}>
                                                                {pricePerUnit != null ? `${formatCurrency(pricePerUnit, currency)}/${volUnit}` : '—'}
                                                            </Text>
                                                        </View>
                                                    </View>
                                                    <View style={s.compactRow}>
                                                        {efficiency != null && (
                                                            <View style={s.compactPill}>
                                                                <Ionicons name="trending-up-outline" size={12} color="#10B981" />
                                                                <Text style={[s.compactTxt, { color: '#10B981', fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold' }]}>
                                                                    {effUnit}: {efficiency.toFixed(2)}
                                                                </Text>
                                                            </View>
                                                        )}
                                                        {tx.vehicleOdometer ? (
                                                            <View style={s.compactPill}>
                                                                <Ionicons name="speedometer-outline" size={12} color={purple} />
                                                                <Text style={[s.compactTxt, { color: textSec }]}>
                                                                    {tx.vehicleOdometer.toLocaleString()} km
                                                                </Text>
                                                            </View>
                                                        ) : null}
                                                    </View>
                                                </>
                                            ) : (
                                                <View style={s.compactRow}>
                                                    {tx.vehicleOdometer ? (
                                                        <View style={s.compactPill}>
                                                            <Ionicons name="speedometer-outline" size={12} color={purple} />
                                                            <Text style={[s.compactTxt, { color: textSec }]}>
                                                                {tx.vehicleOdometer.toLocaleString()} km
                                                            </Text>
                                                        </View>
                                                    ) : null}
                                                    {tx.vehicleMaintenanceItems ? (
                                                        <View style={[s.compactPill, { flex: 1 }]}>
                                                            <Ionicons name="build-outline" size={12} color="#f59e0b" />
                                                            <Text style={[s.compactTxt, { color: textSec, flexShrink: 1 }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                                                                {tx.vehicleMaintenanceItems}
                                                            </Text>
                                                        </View>
                                                    ) : null}
                                                </View>
                                            )}
                                        </View>
                                    )}

                                    {/* Expanded: full details */}
                                    {expanded && (
                                        <View>
                                            <View style={[s.expandDivider, { backgroundColor: `${purple}30` }]} />
                                            <View style={s.expandBlock}>
                                                {isRefuel ? (
                                                    <>
                                                        <DetailRow icon="water-outline" iconColor={isEV ? '#10B981' : '#ef4444'} label={isEV ? 'Energy Used' : 'Fuel Volume'} value={tx.vehicleVolume ? `${tx.vehicleVolume} ${volUnit}` : '—'} colors={colors} />
                                                        <DetailRow icon="pricetag-outline" iconColor="#f59e0b" label={`Price / ${volUnit}`} value={pricePerUnit != null ? formatCurrency(pricePerUnit, currency) : '—'} colors={colors} />
                                                        <DetailRow icon="cash-outline" iconColor="#ef4444" label="Total Cost" value={formatCurrency(amount, currency)} highlight colors={colors} />
                                                        <DetailRow icon="trending-up-outline" iconColor="#10B981" label={effUnit} value={efficiency != null ? `${efficiency.toFixed(2)} ${effUnit}` : '—'} highlight={efficiency != null} colors={colors} />
                                                        <DetailRow icon="navigate-outline" iconColor="#3b82f6" label="Distance Driven" value={distanceDriven != null ? `${distanceDriven.toLocaleString()} km` : '—'} colors={colors} />
                                                        <DetailRow icon="speedometer-outline" iconColor={purple} label="Odometer Before" value={tx._prevOdo != null ? `${Number(tx._prevOdo).toLocaleString()} km` : '—'} colors={colors} />
                                                        <DetailRow icon="speedometer" iconColor={purple} label="Odometer After" value={tx.vehicleOdometer ? `${tx.vehicleOdometer.toLocaleString()} km` : '—'} colors={colors} />
                                                        {costPerKm != null && (
                                                            <DetailRow icon="trending-up-outline" iconColor="#3b82f6" label="Cost / km" value={formatCurrency(costPerKm, currency)} colors={colors} />
                                                        )}
                                                        {tx.notes ? (
                                                            <DetailRow icon="document-text-outline" iconColor={textSec} label="Notes" value={tx.notes} colors={colors} />
                                                        ) : null}
                                                    </>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <>
                                                        <DetailRow icon="cash-outline" iconColor="#ef4444" label="Maintenance Cost" value={formatCurrency(amount, currency)} highlight colors={colors} />
                                                        <DetailRow icon="speedometer-outline" iconColor={purple} label="Odometer" value={tx.vehicleOdometer ? `${tx.vehicleOdometer.toLocaleString()} km` : '—'} colors={colors} />
                                                        {tx.vehicleMaintenanceItems ? (
                                                            <DetailRow icon="build-outline" iconColor="#f59e0b" label="Repaired / Replaced" value={tx.vehicleMaintenanceItems} colors={colors} />
                                                        ) : null}
                                                        {tx.notes ? (
                                                            <DetailRow icon="document-text-outline" iconColor={textSec} label="Notes" value={tx.notes} colors={colors} />
                                                        ) : null}
                                                    </>
                                                )}
                                            </View>

                                            {/* Actions: Edit + Delete – match transaction history style */}
                                            <View style={s.actionRow}>
                                                <TouchableOpacity
                                                    style={s.editBtn}
                                                    onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/add-transaction', params: { editId: tx.id } })}
                                                >
                                                    <Ionicons name="pencil" size={13} color="#6A6FF8" />
                                                    <Text style={s.editTxt}>Edit</Text>
                                                </TouchableOpacity>
                                                <TouchableOpacity
                                                    style={s.deleteBtn}
                                                    onPress={() => confirmDeleteTx(tx)}
                                                >
                                                    <Ionicons name="trash" size={13} color="#dc2626" />
                                                    <Text style={s.deleteTxt}>Delete</Text>
                                                </TouchableOpacity>
                                            </View>
                                        </View>
                                    )}
                                </TouchableOpacity>
                            );
                        })
                    )}
                </View>
                <View style={{ height: 100 }} />
            </ScrollView>
        </View>
    );
}

function DetailRow({ icon, iconColor, label, value, highlight, colors }) {
    return (
        <View style={s.detailRow}>
            <View style={[s.detailIconWrap, { backgroundColor: `${iconColor}15` }]}>
                <Ionicons name={icon} size={13} color={iconColor} />
            </View>
            <Text style={[s.detailLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{label}</Text>
            <Text style={[s.detailValue, { color: highlight ? '#10B981' : colors.textPrimary }]}>{value}</Text>
        </View>
    );
}

const s = StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1 },
    handle: {
        width: 36,
        height: 4,
        borderRadius: 2,
        backgroundColor: "rgba(83,96,132,0.2)",
        alignSelf: "center",
        marginTop: Spacing.md,
    },
    header: {
        flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg,
        paddingVertical: Spacing.md,
    },
    headerIconBtn: { width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 14, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
    headerCenter: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: Spacing.sm },
    headerAvatar: { width: 32, height: 32, borderRadius: 10, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
    headerTitle: { fontSize: FontSize.md, fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold' },
    headerSub: { fontSize: FontSize.xs, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular' },

    kpiGrid: {
        flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap',
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg, gap: Spacing.md,
        marginBottom: Spacing.lg,
    },
    kpiCard: {
        width: '47%', padding: Spacing.md,
        borderRadius: 16, borderWidth: 1,
        gap: 4,
    },
    kpiVal: { fontSize: FontSize.lg, fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold', marginTop: 2 },
    kpiLbl: { fontSize: FontSize.xs, fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular' },

    sortRow: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg,
        paddingBottom: Spacing.md,
        gap: Spacing.sm,
    },
    sortLabel: {
        fontSize: FontSize.sm,
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold',
    },
    sortChipRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 },
    sortChip: {
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.full,
        borderWidth: 1,
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.md,
        paddingVertical: 5,
    },
    sortChipText: {

================ FILE: app/vehicles/index.js ================

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
    View, Text, StyleSheet, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity,
    Modal, TextInput, Platform, Alert, useWindowDimensions, ActivityIndicator,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Ionicons, MaterialCommunityIcons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useApp } from '../../context/AppContext';
import { useTheme } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { Spacing, FontSize, BorderRadius } from '../../constants/theme';
import { formatCurrency } from '../../utils/currencies';

export default function VehiclesScreen() {
    const router = useRouter();
    const { vehicles, transactions, currency, addVehicle, setDefaultVehicle, updateVehicle: ctxUpdateVehicle } = useApp();
    const { colors, isDark } = useTheme();
    const { width: screenWidth, height: screenHeight } = useWindowDimensions();
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

    // Add vehicle modal
    const [showAddModal, setShowAddModal] = useState(false);
    const [newVehicleName, setNewVehicleName] = useState('');
    const [newVehicleType, setNewVehicleType] = useState('gas');
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);



    const handleAddVehicle = async () => {
        if (!newVehicleName.trim()) {
            Alert.alert('Error', 'Please enter a vehicle name.');
            return;
        }
        setSaving(true);
        await addVehicle({ name: newVehicleName.trim(), type: newVehicleType, currency });
        setNewVehicleName('');
        setNewVehicleType('gas');
        setSaving(false);
        setShowAddModal(false);
    };

    const purple = colors.primary;
    const textPrimary = colors.textPrimary;
    const textSec = colors.textSecondary;
    const border = colors.border;
    const cardBg = colors.card;

    return (
        <View style={[s.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingTop: Platform.OS === 'android' ? insets.top : 0 }]}>
            {/* Gradient background */}
            <Svg key={colors.background} style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject} width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} pointerEvents="none">
                <Defs>
                    <SvgGrad id="bgGradV" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                        <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                        <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                    </SvgGrad>
                </Defs>
                <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenWidth} height={screenHeight} fill="url(#bgGradV)" />
            </Svg>

            <View style={[s.handle, { backgroundColor: colors.border }]} />

            {/* Header */}
            <View style={s.header}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => { if (router.canDismiss?.()) { router.dismiss(); } else { router.back(); } }}
                    style={[s.headerIconBtn, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: border, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                >
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={purple} />
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <View style={s.headerCenter}>
                    <View style={[s.headerAvatar, { backgroundColor: purple }]}>
                        <Ionicons name="car-sport" size={16} color="#fff" />
                    </View>
                    <View>
                        <Text style={[s.headerTitle, { color: textPrimary }]}>My Vehicles</Text>
                        <Text style={[s.headerSub, { color: textSec }]}>{vehicles.length} registered</Text>
                    </View>
                </View>

                <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => setShowAddModal(true)}
                    style={[s.headerIconBtn, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: border, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                >
                    <Ionicons name="add" size={22} color={purple} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={s.scrollContent}>



                {/* ── Vehicle Cards ── */}
                {vehicles && vehicles.length > 0 ? (
                    <>
                        {vehicles.map(v => {
                            const vehicleTxs = transactions.filter(t => t.vehicleId === v.id);
                            const refuels = vehicleTxs.filter(t => t.vehicleLogType === 'refuel');
                            const maintenances = vehicleTxs.filter(t => t.vehicleLogType === 'maintenance');
                            const latestOdo = vehicleTxs.reduce((max, t) => Math.max(max, t.vehicleOdometer || 0), v.initialOdometer || 0);
                            const totalCost = vehicleTxs.reduce((sum, t) => sum + Math.abs(t.amount || 0), 0);
                            const volUnit = v.type === 'ev' ? 'kWh' : 'L';
                            const totalVolume = refuels.reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.vehicleVolume || 0), 0);
                            const sortedRefuels = [...refuels].sort((a, b) => new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date));
                            const firstOdo = v.initialOdometer || sortedRefuels[0]?.vehicleOdometer || 0;
                            const lastOdo = sortedRefuels[sortedRefuels.length - 1]?.vehicleOdometer || 0;
                            const totalDist = Math.max(0, lastOdo - firstOdo);
                            const avgEff = totalVolume > 0 && totalDist > 0 ? (totalDist / totalVolume).toFixed(1) : null;

                            return (
                                <TouchableOpacity
                                    key={v.id}
                                    style={[s.vehicleCard, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: border }]}
                                    onPress={() => router.push(`/vehicle/${v.id}`)}
                                    activeOpacity={0.82}
                                >
                                    <View style={s.cardHeader}>
                                        <View style={[s.cardIconWrap, { backgroundColor: v.type === 'ev' ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.12)' : `${purple}15` }]}>
                                            {v.type === 'ev' ? (
                                                <Ionicons name="flash" size={22} color={colors.emerald} />
                                            ) : (
                                                <MaterialCommunityIcons name="gas-station" size={22} color={purple} />
                                            )}
                                        </View>
                                        <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: Spacing.md }}>
                                            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                                                <Text style={[s.vehicleName, { color: textPrimary }]}>{v.name}</Text>
                                                {v.isDefault === 1 && (
                                                    <View style={[s.defaultBadge, { backgroundColor: colors.emeraldLight }]}>
                                                        <Text style={[s.defaultBadgeTxt, { color: colors.emeraldDark }]}>Default</Text>
                                                    </View>
                                                )}
                                            </View>
                                            <Text style={[s.vehicleType, { color: textSec }]}>
                                                {v.type === 'ev' ? 'Electric Vehicle' : 'Gasoline / Diesel'}
                                            </Text>
                                        </View>
                                        <TouchableOpacity
                                            style={s.starBtn}
                                            onPress={() => setDefaultVehicle(v.id)}
                                            activeOpacity={0.7}
                                        >
                                            <Ionicons name={v.isDefault === 1 ? 'star' : 'star-outline'} size={20} color={v.isDefault === 1 ? colors.gold : textSec} />
                                        </TouchableOpacity>
                                    </View>

                                    <View style={[s.cardDivider, { backgroundColor: border }]} />
                                    <View style={s.statsRow}>
                                        <View style={s.statItem}>
                                            <Text style={[s.statVal, { color: textPrimary }]}>
                                                {latestOdo > 0 ? latestOdo.toLocaleString() : '—'}
                                            </Text>
                                            <Text style={[s.statLbl, { color: textSec }]}>Odometer (km)</Text>
                                        </View>
                                        <View style={[s.statDivider, { backgroundColor: border }]} />
                                        <View style={s.statItem}>
                                            <Text style={[s.statVal, { color: textPrimary }]}>
                                                {totalVolume > 0 ? `${totalVolume.toFixed(1)} ${volUnit}` : '—'}
                                            </Text>
                                            <Text style={[s.statLbl, { color: textSec }]}>Total Consumed</Text>
                                        </View>
                                        <View style={[s.statDivider, { backgroundColor: border }]} />
                                        <View style={s.statItem}>
                                            <Text style={[s.statVal, { color: avgEff ? '#10B981' : textPrimary }]}>
                                                {avgEff ? `${avgEff}` : '—'}
                                            </Text>
                                            <Text style={[s.statLbl, { color: textSec }]}>
                                                {v.type === 'ev' ? 'km/kWh' : 'km/L'}
                                            </Text>
                                        </View>
                                    </View>

                                    <View style={s.cardFooter}>
                                        <View style={[s.badge, { backgroundColor: `${purple}12` }]}>
                                            <Ionicons name="water-outline" size={12} color={purple} />
                                            <Text style={[s.badgeTxt, { color: purple }]}>{refuels.length} refuels</Text>
                                        </View>
                                        <View style={[s.badge, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.1)' }]}>
                                            <Ionicons name="build-outline" size={12} color={colors.gold} />
                                            <Text style={[s.badgeTxt, { color: '#f59e0b' }]}>{maintenances.length} services</Text>
                                        </View>
                                        <Text style={[s.totalCost, { color: '#ef4444' }]}>
                                            {formatCurrency(totalCost, currency)} total
                                        </Text>
                                    </View>
                                </TouchableOpacity>
                            );
                        })}
                    </>
                ) : (
                    <View style={[s.emptyState, { borderColor: border }]}>
                        <View style={[s.emptyIcon, { backgroundColor: `${purple}15` }]}>
                            <Ionicons name="car-sport-outline" size={40} color={purple} />
                        </View>
                        <Text style={[s.emptyTitle, { color: textPrimary }]}>No Vehicles Yet</Text>
                        <Text style={[s.emptyDesc, { color: textSec }]}>
                            Track fuel efficiency, maintenance costs and driving metrics for your vehicles.
                        </Text>
                        <TouchableOpacity
                            style={[s.emptyBtn, { backgroundColor: purple }]}
                            onPress={() => setShowAddModal(true)}
                        >
                            <Ionicons name="add" size={18} color="#fff" />
                            <Text style={s.emptyBtnTxt}>Add First Vehicle</Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                )}
                <View style={{ height: 100 }} />
            </ScrollView>



            {/* ── Add Vehicle Modal ── */}
            <Modal visible={showAddModal} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={() => setShowAddModal(false)}>
                <View style={s.modalOverlay}>
                    <View style={[s.modalBox, { backgroundColor: cardBg }]}>
                        <View style={s.modalHeader}>
                            <View style={[s.cardIconWrap, { backgroundColor: `${purple}15` }]}>
                                <Ionicons name="car-sport" size={20} color={purple} />
                            </View>
                            <Text style={[s.modalTitle, { color: textPrimary }]}>Add Vehicle</Text>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                onPress={() => requestAnimationFrame(() => setShowAddModal(false))}
                                style={[s.modalClose, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}
                            >
                                <Ionicons name="close" size={18} color={textSec} />
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>

                        <Text style={[s.inputLabel, { color: textSec }]}>Vehicle Name</Text>
                        <TextInput
                            style={[s.textInput, { borderColor: border, color: textPrimary, backgroundColor: colors.inputBg }]}
                            placeholder="e.g. Toyota Corolla"
                            placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                            value={newVehicleName}
                            onChangeText={setNewVehicleName}
                        />

                        <Text style={[s.inputLabel, { color: textSec, marginTop: Spacing.md }]}>Vehicle Type</Text>
                        <View style={[s.typeToggle, { backgroundColor: colors.inputBg, borderColor: border }]}>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                style={[s.typeBtn, newVehicleType === 'gas' && { backgroundColor: purple }]}
                                onPress={() => setNewVehicleType('gas')}
                            >
                                <Ionicons name="water" size={14} color={newVehicleType === 'gas' ? '#fff' : purple} />
                                <Text style={[s.typeTxt, { color: textSec }, newVehicleType === 'gas' && { color: '#fff' }]}>Gas (Liters)</Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                style={[s.typeBtn, newVehicleType === 'ev' && { backgroundColor: '#10B981' }]}
                                onPress={() => setNewVehicleType('ev')}
                            >
                                <Ionicons name="flash" size={14} color={newVehicleType === 'ev' ? '#fff' : colors.emerald} />
                                <Text style={[s.typeTxt, { color: textSec }, newVehicleType === 'ev' && { color: '#fff' }]}>Electric (kWh)</Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>

                        <View style={s.modalActions}>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                style={[s.cancelBtn, { borderColor: border }]}
                                onPress={() => requestAnimationFrame(() => setShowAddModal(false))}
                            >
                                <Text style={[s.cancelTxt, { color: textSec }]}>Cancel</Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                            <TouchableOpacity
                                style={[s.saveBtn, { backgroundColor: purple, opacity: saving ? 0.7 : 1 }]}
                                onPress={handleAddVehicle}
                                disabled={saving}
                            >
                                <Text style={s.saveTxt}>{saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Vehicle'}</Text>
                            </TouchableOpacity>
                        </View>
                    </View>
                </View>
            </Modal>
        </View>
    );
}

================ FILE: components/DonutChart.js ================

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Circle, G } from 'react-native-svg';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { FontSize } from '../constants/theme';

export default function DonutChart({ data, size = 170, strokeWidth = 24, centerLabel, centerValue, colors }) {
    const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
    const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
    const slices = (data || []).filter(d => d.value > 0);
    const total = slices.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.value, 0);

    if (total <= 0) {
        return (
            <View style={[styles.donutChartWrap, { width: size, height: size }]}>
                <Ionicons name="pie-chart-outline" size={40} color={colors.textSecondary} />
            </View>
        );
    }

    let startFraction = 0;

    return (
        <View style={[styles.donutChartWrap, { width: size, height: size }]}>
            <Svg width={size} height={size}>
                <Circle
                    cx={size / 2}
                    cy={size / 2}
                    r={radius}
                    stroke={colors.border}
                    strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
                    fill="none"
                />
                <G rotation="-90" origin={`${size / 2}, ${size / 2}`}>
                    {slices.map((slice, idx) => {
                        const fraction = slice.value / total;
                        const dash = circumference * fraction;
                        const offset = -circumference * startFraction;
                        startFraction += fraction;

                        return (
                            <Circle
                                key={`${slice.label}-${idx}`}
                                cx={size / 2}
                                cy={size / 2}
                                r={radius}
                                stroke={slice.color}
                                strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
                                fill="none"
                                strokeDasharray={`${dash} ${Math.max(circumference - dash, 0.0001)}`}
                                strokeDashoffset={offset}
                                strokeLinecap="butt"
                            />
                        );
                    })}
                </G>
            </Svg>
            <View style={styles.donutCenter}>
                <Text style={[styles.donutCenterLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>{centerLabel}</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.donutCenterValue, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>{centerValue}</Text>
            </View>
        </View>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    donutChartWrap: {
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        alignSelf: 'center',
    },
    donutCenter: {
        position: 'absolute',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        width: '100%',
        height: '100%',
    },
    donutCenterLabel: {
        fontSize: FontSize.xs,
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular',
    },
    donutCenterValue: {
        fontSize: FontSize.lg,
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold',
        marginTop: -2,
    },
});


================ FILE: components/EdgeDetectionCropper.js ================

/**
 * EdgeDetectionCropper.js
 *
 * Document-crop UI matching FinanceFlow Scan Receipt design:
 *  - Transparent header, ExtraBold XL "Crop Receipt" title
 *  - Top-left back button (arrow-back) → "Use Original"
 *  - Draggable orange corner handles on the image
 *  - "Use Original" (left) + "Crop & Continue" (right) buttons
 */
import React, { useState, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
    View,
    StyleSheet,
    PanResponder,
    Dimensions,
    TouchableOpacity,
    Text,
    Image,
    ActivityIndicator,
} from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Polygon, Line } from 'react-native-svg';
import { FontSize, Spacing, BorderRadius } from '../constants/theme';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';

const HANDLE_RADIUS = 22;
const DOT_RADIUS    = 14;

const EdgeDetectionCropper = ({
    imageUri,
    imageWidth,
    imageHeight,
    initialCorners,
    onContinue,
    onUseOriginal,
    processing,
    colors,
    insets = { top: 0, bottom: 0 },
}) => {
    const { width: screenW, height: screenH } = Dimensions.get('window');

    // ── Layout: fit image to screen while preserving aspect ratio ────────
    const MAX_IMG_H = screenH * 0.60;
    let imgRenderW = screenW - Spacing.xl * 2;
    let imgRenderH = (imageHeight / imageWidth) * imgRenderW;

    if (imgRenderH > MAX_IMG_H) {
        imgRenderH = MAX_IMG_H;
        imgRenderW = (imageWidth / imageHeight) * imgRenderH;
    }

    const vW = imgRenderW;
    const vH = imgRenderH;

    // Convert normalised (0–1) corners → pixel positions on-screen
    const toLocal = useCallback(
        (pt) => ({ x: pt.x * vW, y: pt.y * vH }),
        [vW, vH],
    );

    // ── Corner state ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const [corners, setCorners] = useState(() => ({
        topLeft:     toLocal(initialCorners.topLeft),
        topRight:    toLocal(initialCorners.topRight),
        bottomRight: toLocal(initialCorners.bottomRight),
        bottomLeft:  toLocal(initialCorners.bottomLeft),
    }));

    React.useEffect(() => {
        if (initialCorners && initialCorners.topLeft) {
            setCorners({
                topLeft:     toLocal(initialCorners.topLeft),
                topRight:    toLocal(initialCorners.topRight),
                bottomRight: toLocal(initialCorners.bottomRight),
                bottomLeft:  toLocal(initialCorners.bottomLeft),
            });
        }
    }, [initialCorners, toLocal]);

    const cornersRef = useRef(corners);
    cornersRef.current = corners;

    // ── PanResponder: captures start pos so drag is perfectly smooth ─────
    const createPanResponder = useCallback((key) => {
        const startPos = { x: 0, y: 0 };
        return PanResponder.create({
            onStartShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
            onMoveShouldSetPanResponder:  () => true,
            onPanResponderGrant: () => {
                startPos.x = cornersRef.current[key].x;
                startPos.y = cornersRef.current[key].y;
            },
            onPanResponderMove: (_evt, g) => {
                const newX = Math.max(0, Math.min(vW, startPos.x + g.dx));
                const newY = Math.max(0, Math.min(vH, startPos.y + g.dy));
                setCorners(prev => ({ ...prev, [key]: { x: newX, y: newY } }));
            },
        });
    }, [vW, vH]);

    const responders = useRef({
        topLeft:     createPanResponder('topLeft'),
        topRight:    createPanResponder('topRight'),
        bottomRight: createPanResponder('bottomRight'),
        bottomLeft:  createPanResponder('bottomLeft'),
    }).current;

    // ── Continue: normalise back to 0–1 and call parent ──────────────────
    const handleContinue = () => {
        const c = cornersRef.current;
        onContinue({
            topLeft:     { x: c.topLeft.x     / vW, y: c.topLeft.y     / vH },
            topRight:    { x: c.topRight.x     / vW, y: c.topRight.y    / vH },
            bottomRight: { x: c.bottomRight.x  / vW, y: c.bottomRight.y / vH },
            bottomLeft:  { x: c.bottomLeft.x   / vW, y: c.bottomLeft.y  / vH },
        });
    };

    const getPoints = () => {
        const { topLeft: tl, topRight: tr, bottomRight: br, bottomLeft: bl } = corners;
        return `${tl.x},${tl.y} ${tr.x},${tr.y} ${br.x},${br.y} ${bl.x},${bl.y}`;
    };

    const { topLeft: tl, topRight: tr, bottomRight: br, bottomLeft: bl } = corners;

    const cardBg  = colors.card;
    const border  = colors.border;
    const primary = colors.primary;

    return (
        <View style={styles.root}>
            {/* ── Header — matches Scan Receipt style ───────────────── */}
            <View style={styles.header}>
                {/* Back button */}
                <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={onUseOriginal}
                    style={[styles.backBtn, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: border, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                    activeOpacity={0.75}
                >
                    <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={primary} />
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <Text style={[styles.title, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>Crop Receipt</Text>

                {/* Right spacer mirrors back button */}
                <View style={styles.backBtn} />
            </View>

            {/* ── Image + overlay ──────────────────────────────────────── */}
            <View style={[styles.imageWrapper, { width: vW, height: vH, borderColor: border }]}>
                <Image
                    source={{ uri: imageUri }}
                    style={{ width: vW, height: vH }}
                    resizeMode="stretch"
                />

                {/* SVG overlay */}
                <View style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} pointerEvents="none">
                    <Svg height={vH} width={vW}>
                        {/* Tinted selection quad */}
                        <Polygon
                            points={getPoints()}
                            fill="rgba(255,102,0,0.15)"
                            stroke="#ff6600"
                            strokeWidth="2.5"
                        />
                        {/* Edge lines */}
                        <Line x1={tl.x} y1={tl.y} x2={tr.x} y2={tr.y} stroke="#ff6600" strokeWidth="1.5" opacity="0.9" />
                        <Line x1={tr.x} y1={tr.y} x2={br.x} y2={br.y} stroke="#ff6600" strokeWidth="1.5" opacity="0.9" />
                        <Line x1={br.x} y1={br.y} x2={bl.x} y2={bl.y} stroke="#ff6600" strokeWidth="1.5" opacity="0.9" />
                        <Line x1={bl.x} y1={bl.y} x2={tl.x} y2={tl.y} stroke="#ff6600" strokeWidth="1.5" opacity="0.9" />
                    </Svg>
                </View>

                {/* Draggable corner handles */}
                {Object.keys(corners).map(key => (
                    <View
                        key={key}
                        {...responders[key].panHandlers}
                        style={[styles.handle, {
                            left: corners[key].x - HANDLE_RADIUS,
                            top:  corners[key].y - HANDLE_RADIUS,
                        }]}
                    >
                        <View style={styles.handleDot}>
                            <View style={styles.handleCenter} />
                        </View>
                    </View>
                ))}
            </View>

            {/* ── Action buttons ────────────────────────────────────────── */}
            <View style={styles.btnRow}>
                {/* Use Original */}
                <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.secondaryBtn, { backgroundColor: cardBg, borderColor: border }]}
                    onPress={onUseOriginal}
                    activeOpacity={0.8}
                    disabled={processing}
                >
                    <Ionicons name="image-outline" size={16} color={colors.textSecondary} />
                    <Text style={[styles.secondaryBtnText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                        Use Original
                    </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                {/* Crop & Continue */}
                <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.primaryBtn, processing && styles.primaryBtnDisabled]}
                    onPress={handleContinue}
                    disabled={processing}
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                >
                    {processing ? (
                        <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" size="small" />
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>Crop & Continue</Text>
                            <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle" size={18} color="#fff" />
                        </>
                    )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
        </View>
    );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    root: {
        flex: 1,
        alignItems: 'center',
    },

    // Header — matches Scan Receipt page exactly
    header: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        width: '100%',
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg,
        paddingVertical: Spacing.md,
    },
    backBtn: {
        width: 40,
        height: 40,
        borderRadius: 14,
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
    },
    title: {
        fontSize: FontSize.xl,
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_800ExtraBold',
    },

    imageWrapper: {
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.md,
        overflow: 'hidden',
        borderWidth: 1,
        position: 'relative',
    },

    handle: {
        position: 'absolute',
        width:  HANDLE_RADIUS * 2,
        height: HANDLE_RADIUS * 2,
        justifyContent: 'center',
        alignItems: 'center',
    },
    handleDot: {
        width:  DOT_RADIUS * 2,
        height: DOT_RADIUS * 2,
        borderRadius: DOT_RADIUS,
        backgroundColor: '#ff6600',
        borderWidth: 3,
        borderColor: '#fff',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        alignItems: 'center',
        shadowColor: '#000',
        shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 },
        shadowOpacity: 0.45,
        shadowRadius: 4,
        elevation: 6,
    },
    handleCenter: {
        width: 6,
        height: 6,
        borderRadius: 3,
        backgroundColor: '#fff',
    },

    // Buttons
    btnRow: {
        flexDirection: 'row',
        gap: Spacing.md,
        marginTop: Spacing.xl,
        paddingHorizontal: Spacing.lg,
        width: '100%',
    },
    secondaryBtn: {
        flex: 1,
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        gap: 6,
        paddingVertical: Spacing.md,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.full,
        borderWidth: 1,
    },
    secondaryBtnText: {
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold',
        fontSize: FontSize.sm,
    },
    primaryBtn: {
        flex: 2,
        flexDirection: 'row',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        gap: 6,
        paddingVertical: Spacing.md,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.full,
        backgroundColor: '#ff6600',
        shadowColor: '#ff6600',
        shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 6 },
        shadowOpacity: 0.35,
        shadowRadius: 10,
        elevation: 6,
    },
    primaryBtnDisabled: {
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,102,0,0.5)',
        shadowOpacity: 0,
        elevation: 0,
    },
    primaryBtnText: {
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_700Bold',
        fontSize: FontSize.sm,
        color: '#fff',
    },
});

export default EdgeDetectionCropper;


================ FILE: components/EdgeDetectionModal.js ================

/**
 * EdgeDetectionModal.js
 *
 * Full-screen modal that:
 *  1. Normalizes EXIF orientation via expo-image-manipulator (once, upfront)
 *  2. Runs auto edge detection against the normalized image
 *  3. Renders EdgeDetectionCropper so the user can adjust corners
 *  4. On "Crop & Continue" → crops and returns the cropped image
 *  5. On "Use Original"  → returns the image unchanged (no crop)
 *
 * Key invariant: normalizedUri / normalizedW / normalizedH are shared between
 * the preview <Image> in EdgeDetectionCropper and the perspectiveCorrect crop,
 * so what you see is exactly what you get.
 */
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    View,
    StyleSheet,
    ActivityIndicator,
    Alert,
    Modal,
    Text,
    Platform,
    useWindowDimensions,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import Svg, { Defs, LinearGradient as SvgGrad, Stop, Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import EdgeDetectionCropper from './EdgeDetectionCropper';
import { normalizeImage, detectRectangle, perspectiveCorrect } from '../services/documentScanner';
import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { FontSize, Spacing } from '../constants/theme';

const DEFAULT_CORNERS = {
    topLeft:     { x: 0.05, y: 0.05 },
    topRight:    { x: 0.95, y: 0.05 },
    bottomRight: { x: 0.95, y: 0.95 },
    bottomLeft:  { x: 0.05, y: 0.95 },
};

export default function EdgeDetectionModal({ visible, imageAsset, onCancel, onConfirm }) {
    const { colors } = useTheme();
    const { width: screenW, height: screenH } = useWindowDimensions();
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

    // normalizedImage holds { uri, width, height } after EXIF correction
    const [normalizedImage, setNormalizedImage] = useState(null);
    const [corners, setCorners]       = useState(null);
    const [loading, setLoading]       = useState(true);
    const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!visible || !imageAsset) return;

        let cancelled = false;
        setLoading(true);
        setNormalizedImage(null);
        setCorners(null);
        setProcessing(false);

        const run = async () => {
            try {
                // Step 1: normalize EXIF orientation — this is the single source of truth
                // for both the preview image and the crop math.
                const norm = await normalizeImage(imageAsset.uri);
                if (cancelled) return;

                // Step 2: run edge detection using the normalized URI + real dimensions
                const detected = await detectRectangle(norm.uri, norm.width, norm.height);
                if (cancelled) return;

                setNormalizedImage(norm);
                setCorners(detected || DEFAULT_CORNERS);
            } catch (err) {
                console.warn('[EdgeDetectionModal] setup failed:', err);
                if (!cancelled) {
                    // Safe fallback: use the original uri with guessed dimensions
                    setNormalizedImage({
                        uri:    imageAsset.uri,
                        width:  parseFloat(imageAsset.width)  || 1000,
                        height: parseFloat(imageAsset.height) || 1000,
                    });
                    setCorners(DEFAULT_CORNERS);
                }
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
            }
        };

        run();
        return () => { cancelled = true; };
    }, [visible, imageAsset]);

    // "Crop & Continue" — crop using the same normalized image + its exact pixel dims
    const handleContinue = async (finalCorners) => {
        setProcessing(true);
        try {
            const { uri, width, height } = normalizedImage;
            const cropped = await perspectiveCorrect(uri, width, height, finalCorners);
            onConfirm({
                uri:    cropped.uri,
                width:  cropped.width,
                height: cropped.height,
            });
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('[EdgeDetectionModal] crop failed:', err);
            Alert.alert('Crop Failed', 'Could not process the image. Please try again.');
            setProcessing(false);
        }
    };

    // "Use Original" — pass the original image through unmodified
    const handleUseOriginal = () => {
        onConfirm({
            uri:    imageAsset.uri,
            width:  imageAsset.width,
            height: imageAsset.height,
        });
    };

    if (!imageAsset) return null;

    return (
        <Modal
            visible={visible}
            animationType="slide"
            presentationStyle="pageSheet"
            onRequestClose={onCancel}
            onDismiss={onCancel}
        >
            <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.background }]}>
                {/* Gradient background */}
                <Svg
                    style={StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject}
                    width={screenW}
                    height={screenH}
                    pointerEvents="none"
                >
                    <Defs>
                        <SvgGrad id="cropBg" x1="0.2" y1="0" x2="0.8" y2="1">
                            <Stop offset="0" stopColor={colors.gradientStart} stopOpacity="1" />
                            <Stop offset="0.5" stopColor={colors.gradientMid} stopOpacity="1" />
                            <Stop offset="1" stopColor={colors.gradientEnd} stopOpacity="1" />
                        </SvgGrad>
                    </Defs>
                    <Rect x="0" y="0" width={screenW} height={screenH} fill="url(#cropBg)" />
                </Svg>

                {/* Drag handle */}
                <View style={[styles.dragHandle, { paddingTop: Platform.OS === 'android' ? insets.top + 8 : 16 }]}>
                    <View style={[styles.dragBar, { backgroundColor: colors.textSecondary }]} />
                </View>

                {loading ? (
                    <View style={styles.center}>
                        <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={colors.primary} />
                        <Text style={[styles.loadingTitle, { color: colors.textPrimary }]}>
                            Detecting receipt edges…
                        </Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.loadingSubtitle, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                            Analysing document boundaries
                        </Text>
                    </View>
                ) : (
                    <EdgeDetectionCropper
                        imageUri={normalizedImage.uri}
                        imageWidth={normalizedImage.width}
                        imageHeight={normalizedImage.height}
                        initialCorners={corners || DEFAULT_CORNERS}
                        onContinue={handleContinue}
                        onUseOriginal={handleUseOriginal}
                        processing={processing}
                        colors={colors}
                        insets={insets}
                    />
                )}
            </View>
        </Modal>
    );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1 },
    dragHandle: {
        alignItems: 'center',
        paddingBottom: 4,
    },
    dragBar: {
        width: 36,
        height: 4,
        borderRadius: 2,
        opacity: 0.35,
    },
    center: {
        flex: 1,
        justifyContent: 'center',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: Spacing.sm,
    },
    loadingTitle: {
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_600SemiBold',
        fontSize: FontSize.md,
        marginTop: Spacing.sm,
    },
    loadingSubtitle: {
        fontFamily: 'Poppins_400Regular',
        fontSize: FontSize.sm,
    },
});


================ FILE: components/FastInput.js ================

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { TextInput } from 'react-native';

const FastInput = ({ value, onChangeText, ...props }) => {
    const [local, setLocal] = useState(String(value || ''));
    const typing = useRef(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!typing.current && String(value || '') !== local) {
            setLocal(String(value || ''));
        }
    }, [value]);

    return (
        <TextInput
            {...props}
            value={local}
            onChangeText={t => {
                typing.current = true;
                setLocal(t);
                if (onChangeText) {
                    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                        onChangeText(t);
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            typing.current = false;
                        }, 100);
                    });
                } else {
                    typing.current = false;
                }
            }}
        />
    );
};

export default FastInput;


================ FILE: components/BackgroundTaskMonitor.jsx ================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Platform, View, Text, ScrollView, StyleSheet, TouchableOpacity, ActivityIndicator } from 'react-native';
import { taskManager, TaskStatus } from '../services/backgroundTaskManager';
import { theme } from '../constants/theme';

/**
 * Background Task Monitor Component
 * Displays ongoing and completed background tasks with progress indicators
 */
export default function BackgroundTaskMonitor({ visible = false, onClose }) {
    const [tasks, setTasks] = useState([]);
    const [expandedTaskId, setExpandedTaskId] = useState(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        // Update tasks list
        const updateTasks = () => {
            setTasks(taskManager.getAllTasks());
        };

        // Listen for task updates
        const unsubscribe = taskManager.onTaskUpdate(() => {
            updateTasks();
        });

        // Initial load
        updateTasks();

        return unsubscribe;
    }, []);

    if (!visible) return null;

    const activeTasks = tasks.filter(t => [TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING].includes(t.status));
    const completedTasks = tasks.filter(t => [TaskStatus.COMPLETED, TaskStatus.FAILED, TaskStatus.CANCELLED].includes(t.status));

    return (
        <View style={styles.container}>
            <View style={styles.header}>
                <Text style={styles.title}>Background Tasks</Text>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeBtn}>
                    <Text style={styles.closeBtnText}>✕</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <ScrollView style={styles.content} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                {/* Active Tasks */}
                {activeTasks.length > 0 && (
                    <>
                        <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Running</Text>
                        {activeTasks.map(task => (
                            <TaskCard key={task.id} task={task} isExpanded={expandedTaskId === task.id} onToggle={() => setExpandedTaskId(expandedTaskId === task.id ? null : task.id)} />
                        ))}
                    </>
                )}

                {/* Completed Tasks */}
                {completedTasks.length > 0 && (
                    <>
                        <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Completed</Text>
                        {completedTasks.map(task => (
                            <TaskCard key={task.id} task={task} isExpanded={expandedTaskId === task.id} onToggle={() => setExpandedTaskId(expandedTaskId === task.id ? null : task.id)} />
                        ))}
                    </>
                )}

                {tasks.length === 0 && (
                    <Text style={styles.emptyText}>No background tasks yet</Text>
                )}
            </ScrollView>

            {completedTasks.length > 0 && (
                <TouchableOpacity
                    style={styles.clearBtn}
                    onPress={() => {
                        taskManager.clearHistory();
                        setTasks([]);
                    }}
                >
                    <Text style={styles.clearBtnText}>Clear History</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
        </View>
    );
}

/**
 * Individual Task Card Component
 */
function TaskCard({ task, isExpanded, onToggle }) {
    const getStatusColor = (status) => {
        switch (status) {
            case TaskStatus.PENDING:
            case TaskStatus.RUNNING:
                return theme.colors.primary;
            case TaskStatus.COMPLETED:
                return theme.colors.success || '#4CAF50';
            case TaskStatus.FAILED:
                return '#F44336';
            case TaskStatus.CANCELLED:
                return '#9E9E9E';
            default:
                return theme.colors.primary;
        }
    };

    const getStatusIcon = (status) => {
        switch (status) {
            case TaskStatus.PENDING:
                return '⏳';
            case TaskStatus.RUNNING:
                return '⚙️';
            case TaskStatus.COMPLETED:
                return '✓';
            case TaskStatus.FAILED:
                return '✕';
            case TaskStatus.CANCELLED:
                return '⊘';
            default:
                return '?';
        }
    };

    const statusColor = getStatusColor(task.status);
    const isActive = [TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING].includes(task.status);

    return (
        <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.card, { borderLeftColor: statusColor }]}
            onPress={onToggle}
        >
            <View style={styles.cardHeader}>
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.taskName}>{task.metadata.name}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.taskMeta}>
                        {getStatusIcon(task.status)} {task.status.toUpperCase()}
                    </Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={[styles.statusIcon, { color: statusColor }]}>
                    {getStatusIcon(task.status)}
                </Text>
            </View>

            {task.metadata.description && !isExpanded && (
                <Text style={styles.taskDescription} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {task.metadata.description}
                </Text>
            )}

            {/* Progress Bar */}
            {isActive && (
                <View style={styles.progressContainer}>
                    <View style={[styles.progressBar, { width: `${task.progress}%`, backgroundColor: statusColor }]} />
                </View>
            )}

            {/* Expanded Details */}
            {isExpanded && (
                <View style={styles.expandedDetails}>
                    {task.metadata.description && (
                        <>
                            <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>Description</Text>
                            <Text style={styles.detailValue}>{task.metadata.description}</Text>
                        </>
                    )}

                    <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>Task ID</Text>
                    <Text style={[styles.detailValue, { fontSize: 11, fontFamily: 'monospace' }]}>
                        {task.id.substring(0, 20)}...
                    </Text>

                    <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>Created</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.detailValue}>
                        {new Date(task.metadata.createdAt).toLocaleString()}
                    </Text>

                    {task.startedAt && (
                        <>
                            <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>Started</Text>
                            <Text style={styles.detailValue}>
                                {new Date(task.startedAt).toLocaleTimeString()}
                            </Text>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {task.completedAt && (
                        <>
                            <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>Completed</Text>
                            <Text style={styles.detailValue}>
                                {new Date(task.completedAt).toLocaleTimeString()}
                            </Text>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {task.error && (
                        <>
                            <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>Error</Text>
                            <Text style={[styles.detailValue, { color: '#F44336' }]}>
                                {task.error.message}
                            </Text>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {task.result && (
                        <>
                            <Text style={styles.detailLabel}>Result</Text>
                            <Text style={styles.detailValue}>
                                {typeof task.result === 'object'
                                    ? JSON.stringify(task.result, null, 2)
                                    : String(task.result)}
                            </Text>
                        </>
                    )}
                </View>
            )}
        </TouchableOpacity>
    );
}
